\displaystyle+\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}}({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}))^{\top}({\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}){\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle+\langle{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}(\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}),(({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}-{\mathbf{I}})({\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathcal{R}}\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle.

We have the following facts:

•

Fact 1: Since the ball with radius
U_{\omega}
is convex, and
\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}
belongs to the ball, it holds that

\langle\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathcal{R}}\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle\leq 0.

•

Fact 2:

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

(36)

This follows from the standard principal-angle/SVD relation.

Therefore,

\displaystyle I_{11}\leq

\displaystyle\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathbf{I}}-({\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathcal{R}})^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}}\right\|_{2}\left\|{{\mathcal{R}}^{-1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3U_{\omega}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}.

Similarly, for
I_{12}
, we have

\displaystyle I_{12}=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle=\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1})\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\omega_{t+1}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\|

\displaystyle\leq 8U_{\omega}^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

Summing up
I_{1}
and
I_{2}
and multiplying by 2, we have

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k})-{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k},\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 48\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{36U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+68U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle+6U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\omega_{t+1}^{k}-\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}}\right\|_{2}\|m_{t+1}\|^{2}+16U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

(37)

We bound
\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|
as follows:

\displaystyle\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}

\displaystyle=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}

\displaystyle=\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left(\omega_{t}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)-z_{t+1}^{k,*}

\displaystyle={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*}+z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}+\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\qquad+\zeta\beta(\frac{1}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right).

Squaring both sides, we have

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{x}_{t+1}^{k}\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\widetilde{\omega}_{t+1}^{k}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*}\|^{2}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\zeta\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})\delta_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\frac{\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\left\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\right\rangle}_{I_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{4\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))\|^{2}}_{I_{4}}+\underbrace{4\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}}_{I_{5}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{4\frac{\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{4}}\|(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top})\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\|^{2}}_{I_{6}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle}_{I_{7}}+\underbrace{4\left\|{z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}}\right\|^{2}}_{I_{8}}

(38)

We bound these terms separately.

Bounding
I_{1}
.
At a high level, the contraction behavior of
\|x_{t}^{k}\|
is primarily driven by the term
2\beta\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t}^{k})\delta_{t,L}^{k}\rangle
(i.e.,
I_{1}
in Eq. (
E
)).
Nevertheless, establishing a sharp characterization of this term becomes substantially more challenging under environmental heterogeneity. In particular, Proposition
2
shows that
I_{1}
admits the following decomposition:

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\delta_{t,L}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{11}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{12}},

(39)

recalling that
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}
. Inside
I_{1}
, the main negative drift arises from the term
\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle
. Intuitively, in traditional single-agent or homogeneous environment settings,
\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\approx\langle x_{t}^{k},Ax_{t}^{k}\rangle
, which is mainly controlled by the spectrum of
A
, with the desired property directly assumed in Assumption
1
. However, in the presence of environmental heterogeneity, Assumption
1
does not directly guarantee a negative drift of the
x_{t}^{k}
due to the existence of
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
and its intricate interplay with the heterogeneous
A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}
.
Specifically, (1)
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
is not of full rank, (2) the local head error will be distorted in a different manner due to the product
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}
, and (3)
{\mathbf{P}}_{t}
varies over time.
To address this, we further decompose
I_{11}
as

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}^{*}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{111}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{112}}-\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{113}}.

(40)

The first term of Eq. (
E
),
I_{111}
, is a contraction by Assumption
1
. Specifically,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\leq-2\lambda\beta\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

I_{112}
can be controlled via the principal angle distance between the subspace estimate
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
and the underlying truth
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
. Since

\displaystyle\|A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|\leq\mathbb{E}_{s^{0}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},a^{0}\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}},...,s^{(L)}\sim P^{k}}\left\|{\phi(s^{(0)})(\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}}\right\|\leq 2,

(41)

it holds that

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle\leq 8U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

The third term,
I_{113}
, can be bounded as

\displaystyle-2\frac{\beta}{L}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}x_{t}^{k})^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\right))^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\right)\right)^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=-2\frac{\beta}{L}({\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(42)

wherein the local head error
x_{t}^{k}
and the principal angle distance
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|
are also coupled.
Then,

\displaystyle I_{11}\leq-2\lambda\beta\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(43)

It is worth noting that when
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|=0
, i.e., when
{\mathbf{B}}_{t}
and
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
span the same
r
-dimensional subspace, the second and the third terms in Eq. (
E
) become zero, and Eq. (
E
) reduces to the standard negative drift term. We postpone the characterization of the convergence of
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\|
to Lemma
5.3
.

Recall

\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}),

\quad\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

For
I_{12}
, recall from (
16
) that,

\displaystyle\xi_{t,L}^{k}=\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}.

(44)

Then,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\xi_{t,L}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\right\rangle+2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{121}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{122}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{123}}+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{124}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{125}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\rangle}_{I_{126}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle}_{I_{127}}.

For
I_{121}
, we have under conditional expectation,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\big(\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Big(\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+L_{*,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\Big)\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{L_{*,1}^{2}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+4\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+4\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{L_{*,1}^{2}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{L_{*,1}^{2}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+4\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}.

(45)

Similarly, for
I_{122}
, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|~~~~(\text{by Cauchy Schwartz})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}.

(46)

For
I_{124}
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Big(\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+L_{*,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\Big)\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{L_{*,1}^{2}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{L_{*,1}^{2}}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}.

(47)

For
I_{125}
,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau})(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}.

(48)

For
I_{126}
, we have,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})({\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\frac{\beta}{L}U_{\omega}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

(49)

Summing up the upper bounds of
I_{121}
((
E
))
,I_{122}
((
E
))
,I_{124}
((
E
)),
I_{125}
((
E
)) and
I_{126}
((
E
)), conditioning on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, we get,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[I_{121}+I_{122}+I_{124}+I_{125}+I_{126}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left[48\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)+12U_{r}^{2}\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\right]\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(50)

where the last inequality uses Lemma
D.1
and the tower property.

For
I_{123}
, taking conditional expectation and applying Cauchy–Schwarz, we have

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\right\rangle=2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 2\frac{\beta}{L}\|x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}\|\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|.

For
I_{127}
, similarly,

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=2\frac{\beta}{L}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|.

Then, combining the two displays, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{123}+I_{127}]\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|.

(51)

Putting (
51
) and (
E
) together, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{12}]\leq 3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[48\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)+12U_{r}^{2}\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\right]\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+4U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|.

(52)

Invoking Assumption
1
, putting (
43
), and (
E
), back to (
39
), and taking conditional expectation on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{1}]=2\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\delta_{t,L}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle-2\lambda\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[48\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)+12U_{r}^{2}\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\right]\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+4U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|.

(53)

Apply Young’s inequality, for arbitrary
c_{2}>0
, we have

\displaystyle 12U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\leq c_{2}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|^{2},

we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{1}]\leq-2\lambda\beta{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+c_{2}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+3\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(1+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[48\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right)+12U_{r}^{2}\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\right]\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+4U_{\omega}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|+4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|,

(54)

where the last two terms (i.e.,
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|
and
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]\|
) can be bounded by the fine-grained bounds given by Lemma
I.2
.

Bounding
I_{2}
. By Proposition
2
,
I_{2}
can be written as,

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})\delta_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}x_{t}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+\underbrace{\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\langle x_{t}^{k},\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle}_{I_{21}}.

(55)

For
I_{21}
, similar to the derivations of the upper bounds of terms from
I_{121}
to
I_{126}
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}){\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\rangle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})\rangle

\displaystyle+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3}{2}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+L_{*,1}^{2}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle.

(56)

For the last term in Eq. (
E
), by Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\displaystyle\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\rangle\leq\frac{2\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}2U_{\omega}^{3}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|

\displaystyle=\frac{4U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|.

(57)

Then, putting (
E
) back to (
E
) and then putting the result back to (
E
), and using Lemma
D.1
with the tower property, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+3\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|.

(58)

Applying Young’s inequality, for arbitrary
c_{3}>0
, we have

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|x_{t}^{i}\|\leq c_{3}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}.

We get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{2}]\leq c_{3}^{2}\frac{\zeta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{3}{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}+3\left(18U_{\omega}^{4}+32U_{\omega}^{6}\right)\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{i}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta}{KL}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|,

(59)

where
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}]\|
can be controlled using the fine-grained mixing bound established in Lemma
I.2
.

For
I_{3}
, by Proposition
2
and Lemma
D.1
, we have

\displaystyle I_{3}=

\displaystyle 2\frac{\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\Bigg(\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad+\left\langle x_{t}^{k},\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\right)\right\rangle\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\Bigg(2\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{3}]\leq\frac{2\zeta\beta}{L^{2}}\left(\frac{2U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{2U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right).

(60)

For
I_{4}
, we have,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))\|^{2}=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k})\|^{2}~~~~\text{(by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}x_{t}^{k})\|^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2},

(61)

where the last inequality uses
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\|\leq 1
and

\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}\|\leq 2
by Assumption
4
.
From Lemma
D.1
and the tower property, we know

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(62)

Then, taking conditional expectation on (
E
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 8{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(63)

Therefore, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{4}]\leq 32\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{8U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}.

(64)

For
I_{5}
, by Proposition
2
, under conditional expectation, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}x_{t}^{i}+{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}x_{t}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\omega_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+2U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{i}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{6U_{\omega}^{4}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

The second inequality uses Jensen’s inequality,
\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\|\leq 1
,

\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{i}\|\leq 2
, and
\|\omega_{t}^{i}\|,\|\omega_{t}^{k}\|\leq U_{\omega}
.
The last inequality uses (
62
).
Then, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{5}]\leq\frac{32\zeta^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{i}\|^{2}+\frac{24\zeta^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(65)

For
I_{6}
, we have,

\displaystyle I_{6}

\displaystyle=4\frac{\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{4}}\left\|\left(\frac{1}{K}\sum_{i=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{i}\phi(s_{t,0}^{i})(\omega_{t}^{i})^{\top}\right)\left(\delta_{t,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}^{\top}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 4\frac{\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{4}}\left(\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}\left(\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=4\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}.

(66)

For
I_{7}
, write

\displaystyle g_{t,L}^{k}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\delta_{t,\ell}^{k,\mathrm{act}}\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(\widehat{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\nabla\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}).

(67)

For ease of exposition, we let

\displaystyle g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell},{\mathbf{B}},\omega,\theta):=\left(\widehat{r}_{t,\ell}+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}\omega\right)\nabla\log\pi_{\theta}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}),

(68)

where
\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}=\left(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell},\widehat{a}_{t,\ell},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1}\right)
.
Then,

\displaystyle g_{t,L}^{k}=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},\omega_{t}^{k},\theta_{t}^{k}).

(69)

We have,

\displaystyle 2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*})\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{2\left\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\left(\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*}+(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}(\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle}_{\text{second order remainder}}

\displaystyle-2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-z_{t}^{k,*},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}(\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k})\rangle

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}L_{s,1}\|\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|^{2}-2\alpha\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}g_{t,L}^{k}\rangle,

(70)

where (a) holds by invoking Lemma
D.7
(i.e.,
\omega^{k,*}(\theta)
is smooth). Taking conditional expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}
on both sides and then applying
-{\mathbb{E}}[x]\leq\left|{\mathbb{E}}[x]\right|
to the signed second term yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{7}]\leq\;2U_{\omega}L_{s,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\alpha\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}g_{t,L}^{k}\right\rangle\right|.

(71)

We now bound the second term in (
71
) by connecting the minibatch actor direction to the true policy gradient
\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})
:

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}g_{t,L}^{k}\rangle\right|.

(72)

On the other hand, the true policy gradient can be written by the one-step policy gradient identity as

\displaystyle\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})=\frac{1}{1-\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}_{\nu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\left(R(\widehat{s},\widehat{a})+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}^{\prime})-\phi(\widehat{s}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}|\widehat{s})\right].

(73)

Adding and subtracting
(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})
inside the bracket of (
72
), by Lemma
D.6
, the triangle inequality, and Cauchy–Schwarz, we obtain

\displaystyle\eqref{eqn: first order perturb of the policy}\leq

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\quad+(1-\gamma)L_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|\left\|{\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})}\right\|\quad\text{(Cauchy--Schwarz)}.

To bound the first term, we telescope the inner product inside the absolute value into four differences
D_{1},D_{2},D_{3},D_{4}
by inserting the true TD fixed point, a reference copy at time
t-\tau
on the original Markov chain, and a reference copy at time
t-\tau
on the auxiliary chain:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t}^{k,*},\theta_{t}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t}^{k,*},\theta_{t}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right]\right\rangle

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t-\tau}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})^{\top}\left[\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right]\right\rangle,

(74)

where
\bar{O}_{t,\ell}^{k}=\left(\bar{s}_{t,\ell},\bar{a}_{t,\ell},\bar{s}_{t,\ell+1}\right)
.
The four differences are:

•

D_{1}:=
(row 1)
-
(row 2): captures the
critic approximation error
relative to the true TD fixed point at time
t
under the actor sampling
\widehat{O}
;

•

D_{2}:=
(row 3)
-
(row 4): captures the
parameter drift
between
t-\tau
and
t
on the original actor Markov chain;

•

D_{3}:=
(row 5)
-
(row 6): captures the
original vs. auxiliary actor chain mismatch
at the true TD fixed point and parameters frozen at
t-\tau
;

•

D_{4}:=
(row 7): captures the policy-gradient Markovian sampling error under the
auxiliary actor chain
at the true TD fixed point.

We now bound
D_{1},D_{2},D_{3},D_{4}
in turn.

Bounding
\left|D_{1}\right|
.
By Cauchy–Schwarz and the definition
x_{t}^{k}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}
, we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{1}\right|\leq

\displaystyle L_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|\left\|{\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{t},\omega_{t}^{k},\theta_{t}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t}^{k,*},\theta_{t}^{k})\right)}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2BL_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|^{2}.

That is,

\left|D_{1}\right|\leq 2BL_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|^{2}.

(75)

Bounding
\left|D_{2}\right|
.
Applying Cauchy–Schwarz together with Lemma
D.5
, Lemma
D.6
, and Assumption
3
(i.e., the Lipschitz continuity of
\nabla J^{k}(\theta)
,
\nabla\log\pi_{\theta}(a|s)
, and
\omega^{k,*}(\theta)
), and the perturbations
\|\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbf{B}_{t-\tau}\|
,
\|x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|
,
\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|
, and
\|\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\|
that are established in Lemma
I.3
and Lemma
D.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{2}\right|\leq

\displaystyle 2BU_{\delta}L_{*,1}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+4BU_{\delta}U_{\omega}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle+4U_{\omega}^{2}L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\nabla\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k})-\nabla\log\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k})\|

\displaystyle+2(1-\gamma)U_{\omega}L_{*,1}\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2U_{\delta}+4U_{\omega})L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|

\displaystyle+\left(2U_{\delta}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\delta}U_{\omega}BL_{s,1}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}L_{g}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}L_{J^{\prime}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|.

For the last inequality, we used

\displaystyle\|x_{t}^{k}-x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+U_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|+L_{*,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|.

We also used
\|\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\|\leq L_{*,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|
and

\|\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\|\leq L_{s,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|
.
That is,

\displaystyle\left|D_{2}\right|\leq(2U_{\delta}+4U_{\omega})L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}B\,{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|

\displaystyle+\left(2U_{\delta}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\delta}U_{\omega}BL_{s,1}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}L_{g}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}L_{J^{\prime}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|.

(76)

Bounding
\left|D_{3}\right|
.
Applying Cauchy–Schwarz (pulling
\|x_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\cdot\|{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})^{\top}\|
out of the inner product) and Corollary
3
(to bound the difference of expectations under the original and auxiliary actor chains, with parameters held fixed at
\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}
), we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{3}\right|\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|.

(77)

Bounding
\left|D_{4}\right|
.
Applying Cauchy–Schwarz and then the geometric mixing of the actor reset chain, we have

\displaystyle\left|D_{4}\right|\leq

\displaystyle 2\cdot 2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\cdot d_{TV}\left(\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t,L)}^{k},\nu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes\widehat{P}^{k})^{L-1}\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\gamma^{\tau L}.

That is,

\left|D_{4}\right|\leq 4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\gamma^{\tau L}.

(78)

Putting (
75
), (
E
), (
E
), and (
78
) back to (
E
), we get

\displaystyle\left|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\langle x_{t}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\nabla_{\theta}\omega_{t}^{k,*})^{\top}\left(g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right)\right\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2BL_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+(2U_{\delta}+4U_{\omega})L_{*,1}B{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}B{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|

\displaystyle+\left(2U_{\delta}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\delta}U_{\omega}BL_{s,1}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}L_{g}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}L_{J^{\prime}}\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+\frac{8U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B\,\gamma^{\tau L}.

(79)

Combining (
E
) with (
72
), using Lemma
I.3
for the
\omega
and
{\mathbf{B}}
perturbations and the triangle inequality for
\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|
, and then putting the result back to (
71
), we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[I_{7}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}L_{s,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2(1-\gamma)\alpha L_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{x_{t}^{k}}\right\|\left\|{\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})}\right\|

\displaystyle+4\alpha BL_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\alpha(2U_{\delta}+4U_{\omega})L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\tau\beta}{L}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\alpha(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\tau\zeta}{L}+\frac{8\alpha(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\tau\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+2\alpha\Big(2U_{\delta}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B+4U_{\delta}U_{\omega}BL_{s,1}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}L_{g}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}L_{J^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{8U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\Big)\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]+8\alpha U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B\,\gamma^{\tau L}.

(80)

For
I_{8}
, using the orthonormality of
{\mathbf{B}}^{*}
and the Lipschitz condition of the fixed point
\omega^{k,*}(\cdot,{\mathbf{B}}^{*})
(Lemma
D.6
), we have

\displaystyle 4\left\|{z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{t+1}^{k,*}}\right\|^{2}=

\displaystyle 4\left\|{{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\omega_{t}^{k,*}-\omega_{t+1}^{k,*})}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle=
