and

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}^{\prime}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta}):=\;4\tau c_{\theta}(1+(1-\gamma)BU_{\delta}+2U_{\omega})\left(c\left(U_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{mix},1}+U_{\omega}^{2}C_{\mathrm{mix},2}\right){}+U_{\omega}^{3}C_{\mathrm{mix},3}\right)

\displaystyle{}+2\tau c_{\theta}^{2}C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\bigg(2U_{\delta}L_{*,1}^{2}B{}+4U_{\omega}L_{*,1}^{2}B{}+4U_{\delta}U_{\omega}BL_{s,1}{}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}L_{g}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad{}+2U_{\omega}L_{*,1}L_{J^{\prime}}{}+8U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}BL_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\bigg)

\displaystyle{}+c^{2}\left[48\left(12U_{\omega}^{2}+32U_{\omega}^{4}\right){}+12U_{r}^{2}\left(4+32U_{\omega}^{2}\right){}+56U_{\delta}^{2}{}+30\tau^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle{}+\left(78U_{\omega}^{4}+96U_{\omega}^{6}\right)U_{\delta}^{2}{}+24\left(\frac{5}{2}+3U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle{}+16U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}{}+4cU_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}{}+C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}c_{\theta}^{2}\left(2L_{*,1}^{2}\tau^{2}+2U_{\omega}L_{s,1}+4L_{*,1}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle{}+2\tau cc_{\theta}(2U_{\delta}+4U_{\omega})L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}{}+4\tau c_{\theta}(2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}+4U_{\omega}^{2})L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}U_{\omega}.

Let
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}^{\prime}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})
be a coefficient envelope for the remaining scalar residuals not collected in
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}^{\prime}
,
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}^{\prime}
, or
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}^{\prime}
after using
\zeta\leq 1
,
L,K\geq 1
,
\beta=c\zeta
,
\alpha=c_{\theta}\zeta
, and
\tau L=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
, which gives
m\rho^{\tau L}\leq m\zeta^{2}
.
These are the remaining
\zeta^{3}
-and-higher terms, including the
m\rho^{\tau L}
terms and the
\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{4}
residual; the actor reset-chain residual
\gamma^{\tau L}
is kept explicit.
The preliminary envelope
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}^{\prime}
collects the second-order
\bar{X}_{t}
terms from the one-step perturbation bounds, the conditional-mean mixing bounds, and the projection/QR correction. The envelope
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}^{\prime}
collects the second-order
\bar{G}_{t}
terms. The envelope
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}^{\prime}
collects the second-order scalar residuals with
\sqrt{L}
-,
L
-, or
K
-speedup, and
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}^{\prime}
collects the remaining third-and-higher order residuals.
For the
M_{t+1}
term in (
E
), we invoke the PAD recursion (
F
). Multiplying (
F
) by
6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\zeta/(L(1-\gamma))
and using
1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\leq 1+c_{1}^{2}\zeta
, this contribution is bounded by

\displaystyle 6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\frac{\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}M_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\frac{\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}\left(1+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)M_{t}+\frac{864U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}c}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{3}(1-\gamma)}\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{6cU_{\delta}U_{\omega}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\frac{\zeta^{3}}{L^{3}}\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{6cU_{\delta}U_{\omega}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{3}}{L^{2}}+\frac{\zeta^{3}}{LK}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{6cU_{\delta}U_{\omega}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\frac{\zeta^{4}}{L}.

(86)

After the PAD plug-in, define the final coefficient envelopes

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})

\displaystyle:={\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}^{\prime}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})+6cU_{\delta}U_{\omega}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta}),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})

\displaystyle:={\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}^{\prime}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta}),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})

\displaystyle:={\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}^{\prime}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})+6cU_{\delta}U_{\omega}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta}),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})

\displaystyle:={\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}^{\prime}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})+6U_{\omega}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta}).

(87)

For the
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}
contribution we use
\beta U_{\delta}/(L(1-\gamma))\leq U_{\omega}
to absorb the extra factor
\zeta/(L(1-\gamma))
. With these final envelopes in place, choose
\tau L=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
and use
m\rho^{\tau L}\leq m\zeta^{2}
. Substituting (
E
) into (
E
) gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\left(c_{2}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{3}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}L}+4BL_{*,1}c_{\theta}+c_{4}^{2}c_{\theta}\right)\zeta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{864U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}c}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{3}(1-\gamma)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{2}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\frac{\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}\left(1+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)\right)M_{t}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{(1-\gamma)^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{c_{4}^{2}}\zeta+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}\right)\bar{G}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}+8c_{\theta}U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B\,\zeta\,\gamma^{\tau L}.

(88)

The final envelopes
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,i}
add exactly the higher-order contributions generated by substituting the PAD recursion for
M_{t+1}
and are the coefficient constants used in the lemma statement.

∎

Appendix F
Proof of Lemma
5.3
(Principal Angle Distance Analysis)

Our analysis follows the general roadmap of
[
43
]
, while introducing several key distinctions and technical innovations to address the unique challenges arising from actor updates under Markovian sampling. For example,
[
43
]
controls the Markov noise by directly exploiting the mixing properties of the underlying Markov chains. Such an approach is no longer applicable in our setting, since the policies underlying the conditional expectation
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}
evolve over time and are themselves coupled with past trajectories. Departing fundamentally from
[
43
]
, our analysis instead relies on the perturbed mixing bounds that we develop in Lemma
I.2
.

Recall from Algorithm
1
of the update of
\bar{\mathbf{B}}
that

\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}=\mathbf{B}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}.

Since
\mathbf{B}_{t+1}=\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\mathbf{R}_{t+1}^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

That is,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

For
\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\bar{\mathbf{B}}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}=\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\underbrace{\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}}_{I.1}+2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\underbrace{2\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I.2},

(89)

where the last equality follows from Proposition
2
.

For
I.1
in Eq. (
F
), recalling that
\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}=\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle I.1=

\displaystyle\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\|^{2}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}.

I.2
in Eq. (
F
) is related to the negative drift of the
m_{t}
. Specifically,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}I.2=

\displaystyle\langle{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\mathbf{B}_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\right)x_{t}^{k},

which is true because

\left\langle AB,vw^{\top}\right\rangle=\text{trace}\left(B^{\top}A^{\top}vw^{\top}\right)=w^{\top}B^{\top}A^{\top}v

for any
r\times r
matrix
A
and
r
-dimensional vectors
v
and
w
.
Additionally,

\displaystyle\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t}+{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\right)x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle\leq\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{P}}_{\perp}^{*}x_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}+2U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\right)^{\top}A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}+6U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq-\lambda L\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+6U_{\omega}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

For each
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}=\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}(\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*})\|^{2}=\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}z_{t}^{k,*}\|^{2}=\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z_{t}^{k,*}\|^{2}.

From the analysis in the proof of Lemma
5.2
, we know that

\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|({\mathbf{P}}_{t}-{\mathbf{P}}^{*})z_{t}^{k,*}\|^{2}\geq\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{+}({\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*\top})}{rK}.

So,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{+}({\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*\top})}{rK}.

Thus,

\displaystyle I.2\leq-2\lambda\zeta\frac{\lambda_{\min}^{+}({\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*}{\mathbf{Z}}_{t}^{*\top})}{rK}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(90)

Combining the bounds of I.1, and I.2 of Eq. (
F
), and applying Assumption
5
, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(91)

Recall that
\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\|\bar{m}_{t+1}\|^{2}_{F}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}
,
we have

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}+\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}+12U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\|{\mathbf{R}}^{-1}_{t+1}\|^{2}.

For the first term, we use Eq. (
30
) that
{\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|^{2}\leq(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}}
, and for the remaining terms, we use a crude bound that
\|{\mathbf{R}}_{t+1}^{-1}\|^{2}\leq(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq 2
when
U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq\frac{1}{6}
, then,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+24U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

Moreover, since
U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\leq\frac{1}{6}
, we have
(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq 1+3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}
.
Providing
\frac{\lambda\nu}{r}\geq 3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta
, it holds that

\displaystyle(1-\frac{2\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})(\frac{1}{1-U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}})^{2}\leq(1-\frac{2\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})(1+3U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2})\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}).

Then,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r})\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+24U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|.

(92)

By Young’s inequality, we have for any
c_{1}>0
,

\displaystyle 24U_{\omega}\zeta\frac{1}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|m_{t}\|\|x_{t}^{k}\|\leq c_{1}^{2}\zeta\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

Putting this back to (
F
), we get,

\displaystyle\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}\leq(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta)\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+4\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle~~~~~~~~+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

(93)

Next, we bound the third term of Eq. (
F
) by quantities involving
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}
,
\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}
,
\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2}
, and
\left\|{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2}
.
These terms can subsequently be controlled using the newly established bounds in Lemma
D.1
, Lemma
I.2
, Lemma
I.4
, and Lemma
I.6
.
Specially,

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}+(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle~~~~~(\text{by \eqref{eqn: xi decomp}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle+\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp})\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\right)\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\right)\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{2}}.

(94)

For the first term in Eq. (
F
), we have

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|~~~~~~~~~~\text{(since $\|m_{t}\|\leq 1$)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|^{2}.

Note that

\displaystyle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau}={\mathbf{B}}^{*}_{\perp}({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}),~~~~\text{and}~~~{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}={\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}({\mathbf{B}}_{t})^{\top}-\left({\mathbf{I}}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}({\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau})^{\top}\right).

We have

\displaystyle\|m_{t}-m_{t-\tau}\|\leq\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|,~~~\text{and}~~~\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\|\leq 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|.

Applying similar analysis of the first term of Eq. (
F
) to all other terms in Eq. (
F
), we obtain

\displaystyle\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{2}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp})\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\left({\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\right)\omega_{t}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\!\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}}{2}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t}-m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq\frac{1}{2}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{1}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2},

\displaystyle\langle m_{t-\tau},{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}\left({\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}-{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\right)\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}){\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}+\frac{U_{\omega}^{4}}{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}

Combining the above bounds and plugging them back to (
F
), and taking expectation conditioned on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}\right\|^{2}+\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left\|{\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\|^{2}+8{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big](\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\langle m_{t-\tau},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}(\omega_{t-\tau}^{k})^{\top}\rangle}_{I_{2}}.

(95)

Applying Lemma
D.1
to the two local-noise second moments in (
F
), and using

\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2},\qquad\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|^{2}\leq\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2},

we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\langle m_{t},\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}\xi_{t,L}^{k}(\omega_{t}^{k})^{\top}\rangle

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)U_{r}^{2}+16\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}+\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{\tau}{K}\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+8\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}+I_{1}+I_{2}.

(96)

Putting (
F
) back to (
F
) and taking expectation, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t+1}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}+\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)U_{r}^{2}+16\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{\tau}{K}\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+32\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}+4I_{1}+4I_{2}.

(97)

For the remaining conditional-mean terms, using
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{\perp}^{*\top}{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\leq 1
,
\|{\mathbf{P}}_{t-\tau,\perp}\|\leq 1
, and
\|\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq U_{\omega}
, Cauchy–Schwarz and Lemma
I.2
give

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[I_{1}+I_{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{U_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{mix},1}+U_{\omega}^{2}C_{\mathrm{mix},2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|+\tau\alpha\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha c\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+\frac{\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{2U_{r}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\right)m\rho^{\tau L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{U_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{mix},1}+U_{\omega}^{2}C_{\mathrm{mix},2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\Bigg(\tau\alpha\left((1-\gamma)BU_{\delta}+2U_{\omega}\right)+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha c\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+\frac{\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{2U_{r}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\right)m\rho^{\tau L}.

(98)

where the second inequality uses
\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\leq BU_{\delta}
and
\|x_{t}^{k}\|\leq 2U_{\omega}
.

In addition, applying Eq. (
32
), we have

\displaystyle 2{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{t}\|_{F}^{2}\leq\frac{2U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}.

Taking full expectation on both sides of (
F
), using the tower property, and using
\beta=c\zeta
,
Recall that

M_{t}={\mathbb{E}}\|m_{t}\|_{F}^{2},\quad\bar{X}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2},\quad\bar{G}_{t}=\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}.

Taking full expectation on both sides of (
F
), using the tower property, using
\beta=c\zeta
, and Lemma
I.4
, we have

\displaystyle 4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{\tau}{K}\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 64\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{\tau^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\bar{X}_{t}+4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}\Bigg(64\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}c^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+1024\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+32\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{c^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha^{2}}{L^{2}}+2\frac{c^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg).

Lemma
I.6
gives

\displaystyle 32\tau\sum_{j=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 6144\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\bar{X}_{t}+32\tau^{2}\Bigg(768\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+12288\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\Bigg).

Substituting these displays and the full-expectation version of (
F
) into the full-expectation version of (
F
) yields

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)M_{t}

\displaystyle+\Bigg(\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}+64\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\frac{\tau^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}+6144\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{4\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{U_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{mix},1}+U_{\omega}^{2}C_{\mathrm{mix},2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\Bigg(\tau\alpha\left((1-\gamma)BU_{\delta}+2U_{\omega}\right)+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha c\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\zeta}{L}\left(\frac{2U_{r}U_{\omega}}{1-\gamma}+2U_{\omega}^{2}\right)m\rho^{\tau L}+\frac{4\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{12\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)U_{r}^{2}+48\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+4\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}\Bigg(64\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}c^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+1024\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+32\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{c^{2}\zeta^{2}\alpha^{2}}{L^{2}}+6\frac{c^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle+32\tau^{2}\Bigg(768\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}c^{2}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+12288\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\Bigg)+\frac{2U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+168U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}m^{2}\rho^{2\tau L}.

(99)

Under the single-timescale parametrization
\beta=c\zeta
and
\alpha=c_{\theta}\zeta
with fixed
c,c_{\theta}>0
, define

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta}):=

\displaystyle\;64\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}c^{2}+6144\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta}):=

\displaystyle\;4\tau c_{\theta}\left(U_{\omega}C_{\mathrm{mix},1}+U_{\omega}^{2}C_{\mathrm{mix},2}\right)\left((1-\gamma)BU_{\delta}+2U_{\omega}+1\right)

\displaystyle+4\left[12\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}^{2}\right)U_{r}^{2}+48\left(U_{\omega}+\frac{3}{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\right)\right]

\displaystyle+24\left(\frac{1}{2}+U_{\omega}\right)\tau^{2}c^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}+\left(2+768\tau^{2}\right)U_{\omega}^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}.

(100)

Let
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})
be a coefficient envelope for the terms in (
F
) not collected in
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}
or
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}
after using
\beta=c\zeta
,
\alpha=c_{\theta}\zeta
,
\zeta\leq 1
,
L,K\geq 1
, and
\tau L=\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil
, which gives
m\rho^{\tau L}\leq m\zeta^{2}
.
The terms absorbed into
{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}
are the remaining
\zeta^{3}
-and-higher terms, including the mixing residuals after
m\rho^{\tau L}\leq m\zeta^{2}
.
Then

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)M_{t}+\left(\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}.

(101)

Appendix G
Proof of Lemma
5.4
(Policy Gradient Analysis)

Recall from Eq. (
10
) that the actor uses the minibatch estimator

\displaystyle g_{t,L}^{k}=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\delta_{t,\ell}^{k,\mathrm{act}}\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}).

(102)

By smoothness of
J^{k}(\theta)
and the actor update

\theta_{t+1}^{k}=\theta_{t}^{k}+\alpha g_{t,L}^{k}
, we have

\displaystyle J^{k}(\theta_{t+1}^{k})\geq

\displaystyle J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})+\alpha\left\langle\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k}),g_{t,L}^{k}\right\rangle-\frac{L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\alpha^{2}\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})+(1-\gamma)\alpha\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}+\alpha\left\langle\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k}),g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right\rangle-\frac{L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\alpha^{2}\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}.

Rearranging and taking full expectation gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\leq\frac{1}{\alpha(1-\gamma)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[J^{k}(\theta_{t+1}^{k})-J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right]+\frac{1}{1-\gamma}{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}+\frac{L_{J^{\prime}}}{2(1-\gamma)}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2},

(103)

where

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}:=\left|{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k}),g_{t,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right\rangle\right|.

We first derive a fine-grained bound for the second-order term. By the
tower property,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\mid v_{0:t-\tau}\right]\right]={\mathbb{E}}\left[{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\right].

(104)

Since
\theta_{t+1}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}=\alpha g_{t,L}^{k}
, the second-order
bound in Lemma
I.5
with
j=t
gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\;8(1-\gamma)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\right]+C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]+C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}.

(105)

Combining (
104
) and
(
G
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{L_{J^{\prime}}}{2(1-\gamma)}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\|g_{t,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\;4(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}+\frac{C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2(1-\gamma)}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\frac{\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}}{2}\frac{\alpha}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(106)

It remains to bound
{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}
. The one-step policy gradient identity gives

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})={\mathbb{E}}_{\nu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\left(R(\widehat{s},\widehat{a})+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}^{\prime})-\phi(\widehat{s}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\widehat{a}|\widehat{s})\right].

(107)

Write
\nabla J_{t}^{k}:=\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})
and
\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k}:=\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})
. Using the notation of (
69
), we insert and subtract the true TD fixed-point copy, the frozen-parameter original-chain copy, and the frozen-parameter auxiliary-chain copy:

\displaystyle D_{1}:=

\displaystyle\;{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t}^{k},g_{t,L}^{k}-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t}^{k,*},\theta_{t}^{k})\right\rangle,

\displaystyle D_{2}:=

\displaystyle\;{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t}^{k},\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t}^{k,*},\theta_{t}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J_{t}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k},\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle D_{3}:=

\displaystyle\;{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k},\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k},\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle,

\displaystyle D_{4}:=

\displaystyle\;{\mathbb{E}}\left\langle\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k},\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{t,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J_{t-\tau}^{k}\right\rangle.

Then

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}\leq|D_{1}|+|D_{2}|+|D_{3}|+|D_{4}|.

(108)

We now bound the four terms explicitly before collecting coefficients. The first difference is the critic approximation error. Since

x_{t}^{k}={\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}
,

\displaystyle|D_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq 2B\,{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|\,\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]\leq\frac{1-\gamma}{8}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{8B^{2}}{1-\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}.

(109)

For the parameter-drift term, Lemma
D.5
, Lemma
D.6
, Assumption
3
, and

\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|
give

\displaystyle|D_{2}|\leq

\displaystyle\left[2BU_{\delta}L_{J^{\prime}}+\frac{BU_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)\right]\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|.

(110)

For the original-chain versus auxiliary-chain mismatch, Corollary
3
and the variational characterization of total variation give

\displaystyle|D_{3}|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|.

(111)

Finally, by the geometric mixing of the actor reset chain applied with the parameters frozen at
t-\tau
,

\displaystyle|D_{4}|\leq\frac{2B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\tau L}.

(112)

Define the explicit drift coefficient

\displaystyle\Lambda_{G}:=

\displaystyle\;2BU_{\delta}L_{J^{\prime}}+\frac{BU_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|.

(113)

Combining (
108
)–(
112
), we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-\gamma}{8}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{8B^{2}}{1-\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\Lambda_{G}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|+\frac{2B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\tau L}.

(114)

Lemma
I.5
implies, after taking full expectation and summing over
i=t-\tau,\ldots,t-1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\tau\alpha\,{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha\,{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\quad+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(115)

Applying Young’s inequality to the first two terms on the right-hand side of
(
G
) gives

\displaystyle\Lambda_{G}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-\gamma}{8}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+2\Lambda_{G}^{2}(1-\gamma)\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\left(\frac{\Lambda_{G}^{2}(C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}})^{2}}{4}+\Lambda_{G}C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\right)\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\Lambda_{G}C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle+\Lambda_{G}C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\Lambda_{G}C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\Lambda_{G}C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(116)

Substituting (
G
) into
(
114
) yields

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{t,L}^{k}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-\gamma}{4}{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+\left(\frac{8B^{2}}{1-\gamma}+1\right){\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\Lambda_{G}^{2}(1-\gamma)\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}

\displaystyle+\left(\frac{\Lambda_{G}^{2}(C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}})^{2}}{4}+\Lambda_{G}C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\right)\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\Lambda_{G}C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle+\Lambda_{G}C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\Lambda_{G}C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\Lambda_{G}C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{2B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\gamma^{\tau L}.

(117)

Define the final coefficient envelopes

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}

\displaystyle:=4U_{r},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}

\displaystyle:=16B^{2}+\left(2+C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}}\right)(1-\gamma),

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}

\displaystyle:=4\Lambda_{G}^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}+2\left(\frac{\Lambda_{G}^{2}(C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}})^{2}}{4}+\Lambda_{G}C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\right)(1-\gamma)^{2}+2\Lambda_{G}C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}

\displaystyle:=2\Lambda_{G}C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},\qquad{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}:=2\Lambda_{G}C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}

\displaystyle:=2\Lambda_{G}C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},\qquad{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}:=C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}

\displaystyle:=C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}},\qquad{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}:=C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}

\displaystyle:=C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}},\qquad{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}:=C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}L_{J^{\prime}},

\displaystyle{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}

\displaystyle:=4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}.

(118)

Combining (
103
),
(
G
), and
(
G
), using

\alpha\leq 1
and
16L_{J^{\prime}}\alpha\leq 1
, and absorbing the gradient-square
terms into the left-hand side, gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{\alpha(1-\gamma)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[J^{k}(\theta_{t+1}^{k})-J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right]+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau\alpha}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}\tau^{2}\alpha^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}\alpha}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}\gamma^{\tau L}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(119)

Averaging (
G
) over the agents
first gives the single-time averaged policy-gradient bound

\displaystyle\bar{G}_{t}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{K\alpha(1-\gamma)}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[J^{k}(\theta_{t+1}^{k})-J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\right]+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau\alpha}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}\tau^{2}\alpha^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}\alpha}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}\gamma^{\tau L}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(120)

Taking the time average of (
G
)
from
\tau
to
T-1
and using the telescoping sum in the objective values,

\displaystyle\bar{G}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{K\alpha(1-\gamma)(T-\tau)}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}\left[J^{k}(\theta_{T}^{k})-J^{k}(\theta_{\tau}^{k})\right]+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau\alpha}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}\tau^{2}\alpha\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}\tau^{2}\alpha^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}\alpha}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}\gamma^{\tau L}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}\tau^{2}cc_{\theta}\zeta^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau c_{\theta}\zeta}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}\tau^{2}c^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}^{3}\zeta^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}\tau c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}c_{\theta}\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}\gamma^{\tau L}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where the last inequality uses
|J^{k}(\theta)|\leq U_{r}/(1-\gamma)
and

\alpha=c_{\theta}\zeta
,
\beta=c\zeta
, and
{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}=4U_{r}
. The fixed-agent bound follows from applying the same
time-averaging argument directly to (
G
).

Appendix H
Proof of Theorem
1
(Main Convergence)

Throughout the proof, choose

\tau L=\max\{\lceil 2\log_{\rho}(\zeta)\rceil,\lceil 2\log_{\gamma}(\zeta)\rceil\},

so that
m\rho^{\tau L}\leq m\zeta^{2}
and
\gamma^{\tau L}\leq\zeta^{2}
.
We invoke the three lemma bounds with this value of
\tau
.
We first collect the three estimates that drive the coupled system. Under the
stepsize and mixing-window choices in Lemmas
E.1
,

5.3
, and
5.4
, the local-head
recursion (
E
) gives, for all
t\geq\tau
,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Bigg(1-2\lambda c\zeta+\left(c_{2}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{3}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}L}+4BL_{*,1}c_{\theta}+c_{4}^{2}c_{\theta}\right)\zeta

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{864U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}c}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{3}(1-\gamma)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{2}\Bigg)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{2}^{2}}\frac{c\zeta}{L}+6U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c\frac{\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}\left(1+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)\right)M_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{(1-\gamma)^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{c_{4}^{2}}\zeta+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}\right)\bar{G}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}+8c_{\theta}U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B\,\zeta\,\gamma^{\tau L}.

(121)

The principal-angle recursion (
F
) gives, for all

t\geq\tau
,

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq\left(1-\frac{\lambda\nu\zeta}{r}+c_{1}^{2}\zeta\right)M_{t}+\left(\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}\right)\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}.

(122)

Finally, Lemma
5.4
gives the time-averaged
actor bound

\displaystyle\bar{G}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,2}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,3}\tau^{2}cc_{\theta}\zeta^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,4}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau c_{\theta}\zeta}{(1-\gamma)\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,6}\tau^{2}c^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,7}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}\zeta^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{3}}

\displaystyle+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,8}\tau^{2}c_{\theta}^{3}\zeta^{3}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,9}\tau c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}c_{\theta}\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{G,11}\gamma^{\tau L}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

(123)

We next reduce the two recursive inequalities to their leading-order forms.
Choose
c_{1},c_{2},c_{3},c_{4},c_{\theta}
and
\zeta
such that

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}\leq\frac{\lambda\nu}{2r},\qquad c_{2}^{2}\frac{c}{L}+c_{3}^{2}\frac{1}{L}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}L}+4BL_{*,1}c_{\theta}+c_{4}^{2}c_{\theta}\leq\lambda c,

\displaystyle\zeta\leq\min\left\{\frac{1}{c_{1}^{2}},\frac{\lambda c}{2\left(\frac{864U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}c}{c_{1}^{2}L^{3}(1-\gamma)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\right)},\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{c_{4}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})},\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}\right\}.

(124)

Then (
H
) reduces to

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda c}{2}\zeta\right)\bar{X}_{t}+\left(\frac{36U_{\omega}^{2}c}{c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{12U_{\omega}U_{\delta}c}{L(1-\gamma)}\right)\zeta M_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2(1-\gamma)^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{c_{4}^{2}}\zeta\,\bar{G}_{t}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}+8c_{\theta}U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B\,\zeta\,\gamma^{\tau L}.

(125)

Similarly, (
H
) reduces to

\displaystyle M_{t+1}\leq

\displaystyle\left(1-\frac{\lambda\nu}{2r}\zeta\right)M_{t}+\frac{288U_{\omega}^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}}\frac{\zeta}{L^{2}}\bar{X}_{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta^{2}}{L}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{K}\right)+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{M,3}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{3}.

(126)

Rearranging (
H
), summing from

t=\tau
to
T-1
, and using the telescoping sum together with

\bar{X}_{\tau}\leq 4U_{\omega}^{2}
gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c\zeta(T-\tau)}+\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)M_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4(1-\gamma)^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}}\bar{G}_{T}+\frac{2{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda c(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2{\mathcal{C}}_{X,4}(\tau^{6};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda c(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{2}+\frac{16c_{\theta}U_{\omega}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}B}{\lambda c}\gamma^{\tau L}.

(127)

Substituting (
H
) into
(
H

\gamma^{\tau L}\leq\zeta^{2}
gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}c_{\theta}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)M_{T}+\frac{2{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda c(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4(1-\gamma)L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}L}+\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}(\zeta^{2}).

(128)

Rearranging (
H
), summing from
t=\tau
to

T-1
, and using the telescoping sum together with
M_{\tau}\leq r
gives

\displaystyle M_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2r^{2}}{\lambda\nu\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{576rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\nu c_{1}^{2}L^{2}}\bar{X}_{T}+\frac{2r{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda\nu(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)+\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}(\zeta^{2}).

(129)

Substituting (
129
) into
(
H
) gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{8U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{2r^{2}}{\lambda\nu\zeta(T-\tau)}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}c_{\theta}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}}+\frac{576rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\nu c_{1}^{2}L^{2}}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)\right]\bar{X}_{T}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda c(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4(1-\gamma)L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}L}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{2r{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda\nu(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)\left(\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)+\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}(\zeta^{2}).

(130)

It is sufficient to choose
c_{\theta}/c
,
c_{1}
, and
L
such that

\displaystyle\frac{4L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}c_{\theta}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}}\leq\frac{1}{4},\qquad\frac{576rU_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda\nu c_{1}^{2}L^{2}}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(131)

Then the
\bar{X}_{T}
terms on the right-hand side of
(
H
) can be absorbed into the
left-hand side, yielding

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{8L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,0}}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\zeta(T-\tau)}+\frac{4r^{2}}{\lambda\nu\zeta(T-\tau)}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda c(1-\gamma)^{4}}\left(\frac{\zeta}{\sqrt{L}}+\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{8(1-\gamma)L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,5}\tau c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\frac{8L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,10}c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}L}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{4r{\mathcal{C}}_{M,2}(\tau^{2};c,c_{\theta})}{\lambda\nu(1-\gamma)^{3}}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)\left(\frac{\zeta}{L}+\frac{\zeta}{K}\right)+\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}(\zeta^{2}).

(132)

We now specify a feasible parameter choice. Fix
c_{2},c_{3},c_{4}>0
with

c_{2}^{2}\leq\lambda/4
. Choose constants
c,c_{\theta}>0
such that

\displaystyle c

\displaystyle\geq\frac{4}{\lambda}\left(c_{3}^{2}+\frac{4U_{\omega}^{4}}{c_{3}^{2}}\right),

\displaystyle c_{\theta}

\displaystyle\leq\min\left\{\frac{\lambda c}{4(4BL_{*,1}+c_{4}^{2})},\frac{\lambda cc_{4}^{2}}{16L_{*,1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{G,1}}\right\}.

(133)

Set

\displaystyle\zeta=\frac{L^{1/4}}{\sqrt{T}},\qquad c_{1}^{2}=\frac{\lambda\nu}{4r}.

(134)

With this choice, the first-order contraction and absorption requirements in
(
H
) and
(
131
) are satisfied provided

\displaystyle\frac{2304r^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda^{2}\nu^{2}L^{2}}\left(\frac{72U_{\omega}^{2}}{\lambda c_{2}^{2}L}+\frac{24U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{\lambda L(1-\gamma)}\right)\leq\frac{1}{4}.

(135)

The remaining small-stepsize requirements are collected as

\displaystyle\frac{L^{1/4}}{\sqrt{T}}\leq\min\Bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}},\frac{1}{\sqrt{6}U_{\delta}U_{\omega}},\frac{\lambda\nu}{3rU_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}},\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}},\frac{1}{2U_{\omega}},\frac{1}{c_{\theta}},\frac{1}{16L_{J^{\prime}}c_{\theta}},\frac{LU_{\omega}(1-\gamma)}{cU_{\delta}},\frac{4r}{\lambda\nu},

\displaystyle\frac{\lambda c}{2\left(\frac{864U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}c}{c_{1}^{2}L^{3}(1-\gamma)}+\frac{{\mathcal{C}}_{X,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}{(1-\gamma)^{4}}\right)},\frac{(1-\gamma)^{4}L_{*,1}^{2}c_{\theta}}{c_{4}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{X,2}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})},\frac{144U_{\omega}^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}{c_{1}^{2}{\mathcal{C}}_{M,1}(\tau^{4};c,c_{\theta})}\Bigg\}.

(136)

These conditions are feasible. Indeed, (
H
)
first makes the local-head contraction coefficient and the
\bar{X}_{T}
absorption
coefficient small enough. The choice
c_{1}^{2}=\lambda\nu/(4r)
leaves a positive
contraction margin for the PAD recursion, and (
135
)
then makes the PAD-to-local-head feedback absorbable for all sufficiently large

L
. Finally, (
H
) holds for all
sufficiently large
T
up to logarithmic factors from
\tau
and the displayed
discount/coefficient envelopes. Below we also take
T
large enough so that the
higher-order
\zeta^{2}
residuals are dominated by the leading terms.

It remains to read off the rates. In (
H
),
we keep the scalar residuals with their original
L
-speedups from
(
E
), (
E
),
(
E
), and (
F
),
before they were merged into the single envelope
{\mathcal{C}}_{X,3}
. The constant

L_{*,1}
appears explicitly in the policy-feedback terms in
(
H
);
L_{s,1}
enters only through
the scalar residual envelopes generated by (
E
). Under
(
134
), this gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\frac{1}{c\zeta T}+\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)\zeta TL}+\frac{c_{\theta}^{2}\zeta}{c(1-\gamma)^{4}\sqrt{L}}+\frac{c\zeta}{(1-\gamma)^{4}L}+\frac{c\zeta}{(1-\gamma)^{4}K}+\frac{\zeta^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}}\right),

(137)

where the last high-order term keeps the worst
(1-\gamma)^{-1}
power from
the PAD residual after it is multiplied by the local-head
M_{T}
coefficient.
The displayed powers are the external powers in the recursive bounds; the
remaining dependence through
L_{*,1}
,
L_{s,1}
, and
L_{J^{\prime}}
is not expanded
in this rate display.
Substituting
(
134
) into (
137
)
yields, using
L\geq 1
and
(1-\gamma)^{-1}\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{4}L^{1/4}\sqrt{T}}+\frac{L^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{4}K\sqrt{T}}+\frac{\sqrt{L}}{(1-\gamma)^{5}T}\right).

(138)

Substituting (
138
) into
(
H
), and using
(
134
), gives

\displaystyle\bar{G}_{T}\leq\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{6}L^{1/4}\sqrt{T}}+\frac{L^{1/4}}{(1-\gamma)^{6}K\sqrt{T}}+\frac{\sqrt{L}}{(1-\gamma)^{7}T}\right).

(139)

Thus, before relating
L
and
K
, the actor bound keeps both the local-rollout
speedup and the agent-averaging speedup through the two leading terms

1/(L^{1/4}\sqrt{T})
and
L^{1/4}/(K\sqrt{T})
. If
L\lesssim K^{2}
, then the
agent-averaging term is no larger than the local-rollout term. Taking
L=K^{2}

and assuming that the higher-order term is dominated gives

\displaystyle\bar{X}_{T}\leq\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{4}\sqrt{TK}}\right),

(140)

and

\displaystyle\bar{G}_{T}\leq\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma)^{6}\sqrt{TK}}\right).

(141)

For fixed
\gamma
, this gives the actor stationarity rate

\bar{G}_{T}=\widetilde{{\mathcal{O}}}(1/\sqrt{TK})
up to logarithmic and
coefficient-envelope factors. The
L^{1/4}
speedup before choosing
L=K^{2}

comes from the unavoidable
\alpha/\sqrt{L}
actor perturbation term in
(
H
).

Appendix I
Additional Supporting Propositions and Lemmas

I.1
Proof of Proposition
2
(TD Feature Decomposition)

Proof of Proposition
2
.

It follows from Eq. (
7
) that for any
t
and
L
:

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})=\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}_{t}}{\omega_{t}^{k}}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}_{t}}{\omega_{t}^{k}}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}z_{t}^{k,*}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}z_{t}^{k,*}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}}_{\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}}x_{t}^{k}+\underbrace{\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}z_{t}^{k,*}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})}_{{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}},

where the last equality follows because both
(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}_{t}}{\omega_{t}^{k}}
and
(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}z_{t}^{k,*}
are scalars, thereby establishing Eq. (
23
).

In the remaining proof, we focus on proving the second decomposition in Eq. (
22
).
We have,

\displaystyle\delta_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})=\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}_{t}}{\omega_{t}^{k}}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}_{t}}{\omega_{t}^{k}}\right)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})

\displaystyle-\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left(\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle+\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left(\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=\xi_{t,L}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left(\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)}).

Under steady-state distribution, the
L
-step TD fixed point
z_{t}^{k,*}
satisfies,

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left(\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\right.

(142)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}z_{t}^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})=0.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left(\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+(\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}(\gamma^{\ell+1}\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}x_{t}^{k}\phi(s^{(0)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}x_{t}^{k}\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underbrace{{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]}_{A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}}x_{t}^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}x_{t}^{k},

proving Eq.(
22
).

∎

I.2
Stepback Perturbation

Lemma
I.1
aligns the error terms indexed by
j
to a common reference
time
t
, which is required for a consistent Lyapunov analysis.

Lemma I.1
.

Choose
\zeta\leq\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}
.
Given
\tau^{\prime}
and
t
, for any
k\in[K]
and for any time indices
j
satisfying
0\leq t-\tau^{\prime}\leq j\leq t
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|\right]\leq\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau^{\prime}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right],

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\leq 8(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+128(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+4(\tau^{\prime})^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right],

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|]+\tau^{\prime}\alpha L_{J^{\prime}}BU_{\delta},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}]+2(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\alpha^{2}L_{J^{\prime}}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2},

where
L_{*,1}
and
L_{J^{\prime}}
are defined in Lemmas
D.5
and
D.6
, respectively.

Proof.

For any
t\geq j\geq t-\tau^{\prime}
, we have

\displaystyle\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq 2\|x_{j}^{k}-x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-(z_{j}^{k,*}-z_{t}^{k,*})\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+4\|z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{j}^{k,*}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+8\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{j}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+4\|z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{j}^{k,*}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 8\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+8U^{2}_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}+4L_{*,1}^{2}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+8(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U^{2}_{\omega}(2\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+4(\tau^{\prime})^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2},

where inequality (a) follows from Lemma
D.6
, and the last inequality uses Lemmas
I.3
.

Then, conditioning on
v_{0:t-\tau^{\prime}}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\leq 8(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+64(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+256(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{6}\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{4}+4(\tau^{\prime})^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+128(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+4(\tau^{\prime})^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right].

The last inequality holds since
256(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{6}\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}\zeta^{4}\leq 64(\tau^{\prime})^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}
when
\zeta\leq\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}
.

For the first-moment bound
\|x_{j}^{k}\|
, the same decomposition gives

\displaystyle\|x_{j}^{k}\|\leq

\displaystyle\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}\omega_{t}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}\|+\|z_{t}^{k,*}-z_{j}^{k,*}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|+U_{\omega}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|+L_{*,1}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+2\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+4\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta^{2}+\tau^{\prime}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha.

Conditioning on
v_{0:t-\tau^{\prime}}
gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+2\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+4\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta^{2}+\tau^{\prime}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau^{\prime}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right].

The last inequality holds since
4\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{3}\zeta\leq 2\tau^{\prime}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}
when
\zeta\leq\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}
.

For
\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|]+L_{J^{\prime}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|]+L_{J^{\prime}}\sum_{i=j}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|]+\tau^{\prime}\alpha L_{J^{\prime}}BU_{\delta},

where the first inequality follows from Lemma
D.5
, and the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.1
.
Similarly,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|^{2}]\leq 2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}]+2(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\alpha^{2}L_{J^{\prime}}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}.

∎

Compared with the coarse bounds implied by Lemma
D.1
, the following lemma provides a finer-grained characterization of the upper bounds on

\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|
,

\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|
,
and

\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|
.

Lemma I.2
.

Suppose that Assumption
2
.
Fix
\tau>0
. For any
t\geq\tau
,
L>0
, and
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|\leq\frac{C_{\mathrm{mix},1}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L},

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|\leq\frac{C_{\mathrm{mix},2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+2m\rho^{\tau L},

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|\leq\frac{C_{\mathrm{mix},3}}{1-\gamma}\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+\frac{2U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}.

where

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{mix},1}

\displaystyle=\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}+4U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\max\{1,C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\},

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{mix},2}

\displaystyle=\left(4C_{\mu}+\frac{8}{(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\max\{1,C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\},

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{mix},3}

\displaystyle=\frac{2L_{*,1}+3U_{\delta}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\max\{1,C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\}.

and
C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},\ldots,C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}
are defined in Lemma
I.5
, and
C_{\mu}
is defined in Lemma
D.8
.

Proof.

Recall from Eqs. (
19
) and (
20
) that

\displaystyle{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}).

Recall from Section
D.1
that, for ease of exposition, for any given
\theta
, we write
\mathbb{E}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}
as
\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\theta}^{k},\pi_{\theta},P^{k}}
for short.
The Bellman equation of TD(L) gives

\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(\gamma^{\ell}r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})\right]=0.

In addition, recall from Section
D.1
,
\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k}
,
\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}
, and
\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}
denote the state-action pairs and the corresponding rewards generated under the auxiliary Markov chain, in which the policy remains fixed at
\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}
.
We have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|

(143)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})+(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.
