\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\left.-\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})+(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right\|.

The first difference can be bounded through the Lipschitz continuity of
\omega^{k,*}(\theta)
(i.e., Lemma
D.6
), and the last
difference can be bounded via the geometric mixing of the auxiliary Markov chain to the steady state distribution under Assumption
2
. As for the second difference, we bound the distance between the original Markov chain and the auxiliary Markov chain via Lemma
I.9
. Specifically:

The first difference in Eq.(
143
):
By Lemma
D.6
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(r_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2L_{*,1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq 2L_{*,1}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]

The second difference in Eq.(
143
):
By Lemmas
I.9
and
I.7
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})+(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\rho^{L})(1-\rho)}+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{3U_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

The last difference in Eq.(
143
):
By Lemma
I.9
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\big(\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\big)\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.-\mathbb{E}_{\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}},P^{k}}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(r(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})+(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\right)\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\cdot d_{TV}(\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,L)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,a_{t,L-1}^{k},s_{t,L}^{k}),\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{L-1})

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 2\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\cdot d_{TV}(\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,L)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k}),\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L},

where (a) holds by coupling argument and marginalization, and the last inequality follows from Assumption
2
and the fact that each round consists of
L
local state-action pairs.

Combining the above three upper bounds, we get

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{*,1}+\frac{3U_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]+\frac{2U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2L_{*,1}+\frac{3U_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\tau\left((1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\left.+\frac{C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\frac{C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha^{2}+\frac{C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+\frac{C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)+\frac{2U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
I.5
.
Note that

\displaystyle 2L_{*,1}+\frac{3U_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2L_{*,1}+3U_{\delta}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}.

By setting
C_{\mathrm{mix},3}
as

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{mix},3}=\frac{2L_{*,1}+3U_{\delta}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\max\{1,C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\}.

the definition of
C_{\mathrm{mix},3}
, the last display can be further simplified as

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[{\mathbf{b}}_{t,L}^{k}]\|\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\mathrm{mix},3}}{1-\gamma}\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+\frac{2U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}.

Recall
from Eq. (
17
) that

\displaystyle\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k});\quad\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

Then, we can similarly decompose the difference into three differences.

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]}\right\|

(144)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]}\right\|

\displaystyle+\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]}\right\|

\displaystyle+\Bigg\|{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\Bigg\|.

The first difference in Eq.(
144
):

By Lemma
I.7
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}\frac{3}{(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right].

The second difference in Eq.(
144
):

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\widetilde{r}_{t,\ell}^{k}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}d_{TV}(\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,L)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,a_{t,L-1}^{k},s_{t,L}^{k}),\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{L-1})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}d_{TV}(\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,L)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k}),\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}.

The third difference in Eq.(
144
):

\displaystyle\Bigg\|{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\Bigg\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\Bigg({\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell^{\prime})},s^{(\ell^{\prime}+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell}\left[r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell^{\prime})},s^{(\ell^{\prime}+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell}\left[r(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\Bigg)\Bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle U_{r}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[2d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{\ell},\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{\ell})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle U_{r}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[2d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})+2\ell\sup_{s}d_{TV}(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}(\cdot|s),\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\cdot|s))\right]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle U_{r}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}(2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})\right]+\ell L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right])

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right]+\frac{U_{r}\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right],

where inequality (a) follows from Assumption
3
, and the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.8
and the fact that
\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\ell\leq\frac{\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\left\|{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}]}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right]+\frac{U_{r}\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right]+\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}

\displaystyle+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}\frac{3}{(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{U_{r}\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}\frac{3}{(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]+\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}+4U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}(1-\rho)^{2}}\tau\Bigg((1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]+C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha^{2}+C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg)+\frac{2U_{r}}{1-\gamma}m\rho^{\tau L},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
I.5
.
Setting
C_{\mathrm{mix},1}
as

\displaystyle C_{\mathrm{mix},1}=\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}+4U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\rho)^{2}}\max\{1,C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\},

we obtain the upper bound on
\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{b}_{t,L}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|
as stated in Lemma
I.2
.

Recall
from Eq. (
19
) that

\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}=\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top},

and

A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right].

Similar to the above derivation, we consider the following decomposition

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\Big\|=\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}

(145)

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]\Big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]\Big\|

\displaystyle+\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\Big\|

\displaystyle+\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\big]\Big\|.

The first difference in Eq.(
145
):

By Lemma
I.7
, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(s_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{6}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

The second difference in Eq.(
145
):
By Asssumption
2
, we have

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})(\gamma^{L}\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})-\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k}))^{\top}\big]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2m\rho^{\tau L}.

The third difference in Eq.(
145
):

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right]\Big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\right]\Big\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Big\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell^{\prime})},s^{(\ell^{\prime}+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k},(a^{(\ell^{\prime})},s^{(\ell^{\prime}+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell^{\prime}\leq\ell}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)}))^{\top}\right]\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{\ell},\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\otimes(\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})^{\ell})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}^{k},\mu_{\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k})+\ell\sup_{s}d_{TV}(\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}(\cdot|s),\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(\cdot|s))\right)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle 4\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(C_{\mu}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right]+\frac{\ell}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}-\theta_{t}^{k}\|\right].

where inequality (a) follows from Lemma
D.8
and Assumption
3
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\Big\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\Big\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+\frac{6}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]+2m\rho^{\tau L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2\gamma}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+\frac{6}{(1-\gamma)(1-\rho)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\quad+C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg)+2m\rho^{\tau L},

where the last inequaltiy follows from Lemma
I.5
.
By the definition of
C_{\mathrm{mix},2}
, the last display can be further simplified as

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\big[\widetilde{A}_{t,L}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{t}^{k}}^{k}\big]\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{C_{\mathrm{mix},2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\Bigg((1-\gamma)\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\||v_{0:t-\tau}\right]+\tau\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}+\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+\frac{\tau\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}\Bigg)+2m\rho^{\tau L}.

∎

I.3
One Round and Multi-Round Perturbation

Lemma I.3
.

Fix
\tau\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
When
\frac{\zeta U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}{L}\leq 1/2
,
for any
t\geq\tau>0
and
k\in[K]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta,

\displaystyle\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\leq\tau\alpha BU_{\delta},

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{t}-\mathbf{B}_{t-\tau}\|\leq 2\tau\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}+4\tau\zeta^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}.

Proof.

For any
t\geq\tau>0
and
k\in[K]
, we have

\displaystyle\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|=\|\omega_{t}^{k}-\omega_{t-1}^{k}+\omega_{t-1}^{k}-\omega_{t-2}^{k}+\omega_{t-2}^{k}+...-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\omega_{i+1}^{k}-\omega_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{=}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}\left(\omega^{k}_{i}+\beta\frac{1}{L}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\top}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})\right)-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{i}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\overset{(b)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\beta\frac{1}{L}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\mathbf{B}_{i}^{\top}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\overset{(c)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{\beta}{L}|\delta_{i,L}^{k}|\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta,

where equalty (a) holds because
\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{i}^{k})=\omega_{i}^{k}
, inequality (b) follows from the non-expansive property of projection,
inequality (c) follows from Assumption
4
together with the orthonormality of
\mathbf{B}_{i}
,
and the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.1
.
In addition, by the actor update,

\displaystyle\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\leq\alpha\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\delta_{i,\ell}^{k,\mathrm{act}}\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{i}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{i,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{i,\ell}^{k})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq\alpha\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}|\delta_{i,\ell}^{k,\mathrm{act}}|\|\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{i}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{i,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{i,\ell}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau\alpha BU_{\delta},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.1
and Assumption
3
.

For
{\mathbf{B}}
, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\leq\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|=\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\bar{{\mathbf{B}}}_{i+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|(\mathbf{B}_{i}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|(\mathbf{B}_{i}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}+{\mathbf{B}}_{i}{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}\|\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}\|+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}\|+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|{\mathbf{R}}_{i+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}}+\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2},

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.2
, and
{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}
is defined in Eq. (
28
) as

\displaystyle{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}=\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i,0}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}.

We have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\delta_{i,L}^{k}\phi(s_{i}^{k})(\omega_{i}^{k})^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|\delta_{i,L}^{k}|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{i,\perp}^{\top}\|\|\phi(s_{i}^{k})\|\|\omega_{i}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}.

(146)

When
\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\leq 1/2
,
1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{i}\|_{F}^{2}\geq 1/2
.
Therefore, we have

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{t}-{\mathbf{B}}_{t-\tau}\|\leq

\displaystyle 2\tau\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}+4\tau\zeta^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}.

∎

Lemma I.4
.

Fix
\tau\in{\mathbb{N}}
.
Choose
\zeta\leq\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}
.
For any
k\in[K]
and any
t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, where
t\geq\tau
, it holds that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{2\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+8\tau\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta\zeta}{L^{2}}+2\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\frac{\beta\alpha}{L}+\frac{2\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta}{L}.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 64\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{4}+1024\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+32\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}\alpha^{2}}{L^{2}}+{\frac{16\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

where
L_{*,1}
is defined in Lemma
D.6
.

Proof.

For any
j
, one has

\displaystyle\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|=\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{j,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k}))-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|\qquad\qquad\text{(by non-expansive property of projection)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\right)\|~~~~~~\text{(by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\frac{2\beta}{L}}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{\beta}{L}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|.

(147)

By Lemma
D.1
, we know that
\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|\leq\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta}{L}
.
By Lemma
I.1
, for any
t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, where
t\geq\tau
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|\leq\frac{2\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta}{L}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2\beta}{L}\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right)+\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta}{L}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{2\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}+8\tau\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{2}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta\zeta}{L^{2}}+2\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\frac{\beta\alpha}{L}+\frac{2\beta}{L}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+\frac{U_{\delta}}{1-\gamma}\frac{\beta}{L}.

Similarly, for any
j
such that
t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}={\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k}+\frac{\beta}{L}\delta_{j,L}^{k}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k}))-\Pi_{U_{\omega}}(\omega_{j}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\frac{\beta}{L}{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})\|^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}\left(\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\right)\|^{2}\quad\quad~~~~~~\text{(by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}^{\top}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\frac{8\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|^{2}~~~~\text{since $\|\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}\|\leq 2$}.

(148)

By Lemma
D.1
, we have
\|{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\|^{2}\leq\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}
.
In addition, by Lemma
I.1
,
(
I.3
) can be written as, we further bound
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}
as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|\omega_{j+1}^{k}-\omega_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\leq{\frac{8\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+\frac{2\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 64\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{4}+1024\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}\beta^{2}+32\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\beta^{2}\alpha^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+{\frac{16\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+2\frac{\beta^{2}}{L^{2}}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

∎

Lemma I.5
.

Choose
\zeta\leq\frac{L(1-\gamma)}{2U_{\delta}U_{\omega}}
.
Fix
\tau
.
For
t-\tau\leq j\leq t
,
k\in[K]
,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|\right]\leq(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]+C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha^{2}+C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}},

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\leq 8(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\right]+C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]+C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau^{2}\alpha^{4}+C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)}.

where

\displaystyle C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=2B,C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}=2BU_{\delta},C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}=8BU_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{2},

\displaystyle C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}BU_{\delta}+2B^{2}L_{*,1}U_{\delta}+BU_{\delta}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right),

\displaystyle C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=3BU_{\delta},C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}=4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|,

\displaystyle C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=32B^{2},C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}=128B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2},C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}=2048B^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}U_{\delta}^{2},

\displaystyle C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=8(1-\gamma)^{2}L_{J^{\prime}}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}+64B^{4}L_{*,1}^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}+4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)^{2},

\displaystyle C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}

\displaystyle=128B^{3}U_{\delta}^{3}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|,C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}=48B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}.

Proof.

Recall from Eq.(
69
) that, for each
j
such that
t-\tau\leq j\leq t
,
g_{j,L}^{k}
is defined in as

\displaystyle g_{j,L}^{k}=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\delta_{j,\ell}^{k,\mathrm{act}}\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{j}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})=\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k}),

and that
\theta^{k}
is updated as

\displaystyle\theta_{j+1}^{k}=\theta_{j}^{k}+\alpha g_{j,L}^{k}

Adding and subtracting the one-step policy-gradient target gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|\right]\leq(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right]+\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|g_{j,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right].

(149)

For the second term in (
149
),
we insert and subtract the true TD fixed point, the frozen-parameter original-chain copy at time
t-\tau
, and the frozen-parameter auxiliary-chain copy at time
t-\tau
, yielding

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|g_{j,L}^{k}-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right]\leq D_{1}+D_{2}+D_{3}+D_{4},

(150)

where

\displaystyle D_{1}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})\right)\right\|\right],

\displaystyle D_{2}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)-(1-\gamma)\left(\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\right\|\right],

\displaystyle D_{3}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle D_{4}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|\right].

Recall from Eq.(
68
) that

g(\widehat{O}^{k}_{j,\ell},{\mathbf{B}},\omega,\theta)=\left(\widehat{r}_{j,\ell}+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}^{k}_{j,\ell+1})-\phi(\widehat{s}^{k}_{j,\ell}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}\omega\right)\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta}(\widehat{a}^{k}_{j,\ell}|\widehat{s}^{k}_{j,\ell}).

The first difference is the critic approximation error. Since

x_{j}^{k}={\mathbf{B}}_{j}\omega_{j}^{k}-{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{j}^{k,*}
, we have

\displaystyle D_{1}=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left((\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}x_{j}^{k}\right)\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{j}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left|(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}x_{j}^{k}\right|\left\|\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{j}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}2B{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|\right]=2B{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|\right],

where we used Assumption
3
and the fact that
\|\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}^{\prime})-\phi(\widehat{s})\|\leq 2
.

Intuitively,
D_{2}
captures the stepback difference. By the triangle inequality, we have

\displaystyle D_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|\right]

\displaystyle+(1-\gamma){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\|\right].

For each
\ell
, inserting and subtracting

g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})
gives

\displaystyle\left\|g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left|(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}(\omega_{j}^{k,*}-\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*})\right|\left\|\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{j}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})\right\|

\displaystyle+\left|\widehat{r}_{j,\ell}^{k}+(\gamma\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k})-\phi(\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k}))^{\top}{\mathbf{B}}^{*}\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*}\right|\left\|\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{j}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})-\nabla_{\theta}\log\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}}(\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k}|\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k})\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2BL_{*,1}\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|+U_{\delta}L_{g}\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|,

where the last inequality follows from Assumption
3
and Lemma
D.6
.
Moreover, by Lemma
D.5
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\|\leq L_{J^{\prime}}\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|.

Therefore,

\displaystyle D_{2}\leq

\displaystyle(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}){\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}})\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}})\tau\alpha BU_{\delta},

where the last inequality uses
\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}=\alpha g_{i,L}^{k}
and
\|g_{i,L}^{k}\|\leq BU_{\delta}
.

D_{3}
captures the difference between the original chain and auxiliary chain under actor simulator
\widehat{P}^{k}
.
Applying the TV variational bound and then Corollary
4
to each per-
\ell
tuple, we have

\displaystyle D_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}2BU_{\delta}\,d_{TV}\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(j,\ell)}^{k}(\widehat{s}_{j,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{j,0}^{k},\widehat{s}_{j,\ell}^{k},\widehat{a}_{j,\ell}^{k},\widehat{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k}),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(j,\ell)}^{k}(\bar{s}_{j,0}^{k},\bar{a}_{j,0}^{k},\bar{s}_{j,\ell}^{k},\bar{a}_{j,\ell}^{k},\bar{s}_{j,\ell+1}^{k})\Big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{BU_{\delta}}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\Bigg(2d_{TV}\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot)\Big)+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle BU_{\delta}\Bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{j-(t-\tau)}(\gamma^{L})^{m-1}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}\left[\|\theta_{j-m}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle BU_{\delta}\Bigg(\sum_{m=1}^{j-(t-\tau)}(\gamma^{L})^{m-1}\frac{1}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-m-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle BU_{\delta}\Bigg(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle BU_{\delta}\left(\frac{1}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4BU_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right],

where inequality (a) is obtained by unrolling the block-start TV distance, i.e.,
d_{TV}\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot)\Big)
, backwards using the last two bounds in Corollary
4
. This backward unrolling terminates at the base case

\displaystyle d_{TV}\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:t-\tau}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:t-\tau}^{k}(\cdot)\Big)=0,

since
v_{0:t-\tau}=\widetilde{v}_{0:t-\tau}
.
Applying the same crude bound of
\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]
as in
D_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle D_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4\alpha B^{2}U^{2}_{\delta}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\tau.

Finally, for the auxiliary-chain term, the one-step policy-gradient identity gives the stationary expectation as
(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})
. Thus,

\displaystyle D_{4}\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\right\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]}.

Expanding the square gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{L^{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+\frac{2}{L^{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Big[\Big\langle g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}),

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell^{\prime}}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\Big\rangle\Big]

\displaystyle\overset{(a)}{\leq}

\displaystyle\frac{4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L}+\frac{2B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\sum_{\ell^{\prime}=\ell+1}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell^{\prime}-\ell}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L}\frac{6}{1-\gamma},

where inequality (a) holds because that

\displaystyle\left\|g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\left(\|g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\|^{2}+(1-\gamma)^{2}\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\leq 4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2},

and because of the towering argument of conditional expectation.

\displaystyle D_{4}\leq 3BU_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}}.

Putting the four bounds back into (
150
), and subsequently into (
149
), together with Lemma
I.1
, yields

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right]+2\alpha B{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|\right]+\alpha^{2}\tau BU_{\delta}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{4\alpha^{2}\tau B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+3\alpha BU_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right]+2\alpha B\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{2}\tau BU_{\delta}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{4\alpha^{2}\tau B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+3\alpha BU_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}},

Applying Lemma
I.1
to
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|\right]
to relate to
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+(1-\gamma)\tau\alpha^{2}L_{J^{\prime}}BU_{\delta}

\displaystyle+2\alpha B\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]\right)

\displaystyle+\alpha^{2}\tau BU_{\delta}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)+\frac{4\alpha^{2}\tau B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|+3\alpha BU_{\delta}\sqrt{\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}},

By the definitions of
C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}},\ldots,C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}
, the above bound can be simplified as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|\right]\leq

\displaystyle(1-\gamma)\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|\right]+C_{1}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right]+C_{2}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\beta}{L(1-\gamma)}

\displaystyle+C_{3}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha\zeta}{L(1-\gamma)}+C_{4}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau\alpha^{2}+C_{5}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\alpha}{\sqrt{L(1-\gamma)}}+C_{6}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}.

Simialry, we bound
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]
, where
t-\tau\leq j\leq t
as follows:

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Bigg[\Bigg\|(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)-(1-\gamma)\left(\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\Bigg\|^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Bigg[\Bigg\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)

\displaystyle-(1-\gamma)\left(\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\Bigg\|^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Bigg[\Bigg\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)

\displaystyle-(1-\gamma)\left(\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\Bigg\|^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle+8\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+8\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right].

The first difference in Eq.(
I.3
), which captures the squared critic-approximation difference, can be bounded as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}_{j},\omega_{j}^{k},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})\right)\right\|^{2}\right]\leq 4B^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right].

The second difference in Eq.(
I.3
) can be bounded by

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\Bigg[\Bigg\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\left(g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{j}^{k,*},\theta_{j}^{k})-g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-(1-\gamma)\left(\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})-\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right)\Bigg\|^{2}\Bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)^{2}.

The third difference in Eq.(
I.3
), which quantifies the original-chain and auxiliary-chain deviation for the actor sampling, can be bounded by first applying the total variation variational bound to the squared norm and then invoking the same five-tuple TV bound as used for
D_{3}
in Eq.(
150
), yielding

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\widehat{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{4B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\Bigg(2d_{TV}\Big(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:j}^{k}(\cdot)\Big)+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{j-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{16\tau\alpha B^{3}U_{\delta}^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|.

Finally, we bound the last difference in Eq.(
I.3
) as follows:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\left\|\frac{1}{L}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}g(\bar{O}_{j,\ell}^{k},{\mathbf{B}}^{*},\omega_{t-\tau}^{k,*},\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})-(1-\gamma)\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t-\tau}^{k})\right\|^{2}\right]\leq\frac{6B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)}.

Putting the four bounds together, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 4(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{j}^{k})\|^{2}\right]+16\alpha^{2}B^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{4}\tau^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)^{2}+\frac{128\alpha^{3}\tau B^{3}U_{\delta}^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle+48\alpha^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}.

Applying Lemma
I.1
to relate
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]
with
{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\right]+8(1-\gamma)^{2}\tau^{2}\alpha^{4}L_{J^{\prime}}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}

\displaystyle+16\alpha^{2}B^{2}\Bigg(8\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+128\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{4}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+4\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+4\alpha^{4}\tau^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\left(2BL_{*,1}+U_{\delta}L_{g}+(1-\gamma)L_{J^{\prime}}\right)^{2}+\frac{128\alpha^{3}\tau B^{3}U_{\delta}^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|

\displaystyle+48\alpha^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{1}{L(1-\gamma)}.

By the definitions of
C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}},\ldots,C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}
, the above bound can be simplified as

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\theta_{j+1}^{k}-\theta_{j}^{k}\|^{2}\right]\leq

\displaystyle 8(1-\gamma)^{2}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|\nabla J^{k}(\theta_{t}^{k})\|^{2}\right]+C_{7}^{\mathrm{act}}\alpha^{2}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right]+C_{8}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}\beta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}

\displaystyle+C_{9}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau^{2}\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{10}^{\mathrm{act}}\tau^{2}\alpha^{4}+C_{11}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\tau\alpha^{3}}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}+C_{12}^{\mathrm{act}}\frac{\alpha^{2}}{L(1-\gamma)}.

∎

Lemma I.6
.

Fix
\tau>0
.
Choosing
\zeta
so that
\zeta\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\leq 1/2
and
U_{\omega}\zeta\leq 1/2
,
for any
t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, where
t\geq\tau
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|]\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{L}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+4\tau\frac{U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)}\beta\zeta+16\tau\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle+4\tau U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\frac{\alpha\zeta}{L}+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}\leq

\displaystyle 768\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}+12288\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{4}+384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+192U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}.

Proof.

Fix
\tau>0
. Recall that
{\mathbf{P}}_{t,\perp}={\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}{\mathbf{B}}_{t,\perp}^{\top}
.
For any
t-\tau\leq j\leq t-1
, the one-step perturbation of
{\mathbf{B}}_{j}
can be bounded as

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|=\|\bar{{\mathbf{B}}}_{j+1}{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}_{j}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}_{j}+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}){\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}+{\mathbf{B}}_{j}{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|\left\|{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}\right\|+\|{\mathbf{R}}_{j+1}^{-1}-{\mathbf{I}}\|\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}+4\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2},

(151)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.2
. From (
28
), we know that

\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}\leq\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\zeta.
When
\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\zeta\leq 1/2
, it holds that
\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}\leq 2
.
Thus, to bound
\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|
, it remains to bound
\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|
, for which we have

\displaystyle\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left({\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}+{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}\right)(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|~~~~\text{by Proposition \ref{prop: key intermediate}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|+\frac{\zeta}{KL}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta,

(152)

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.1
. Plugging (
I.3
) into (
I.3
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|\leq 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}.

(153)

Finally, taking the conditional expectation and applying Lemma
I.1
to relate
x_{j}^{k}
to
x_{t}^{k}
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\mathbf{B}_{j+1}-\mathbf{B}_{j}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\beta+4\tau\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta+\tau L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\alpha+{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|\right)

\displaystyle+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}U_{\omega}^{2}\zeta^{2}\quad\quad~~~~~\text{(applying Lemma \ref{lmm: jth to tth error bound})}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 4U_{\omega}\frac{\zeta}{L}\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|+4\tau\frac{U_{\omega}U_{\delta}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)}\beta\zeta+16\tau\frac{U_{\delta}U_{\omega}^{3}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)}\zeta^{2}+4\tau U_{\omega}L_{*,1}BU_{\delta}\frac{\alpha\zeta}{L}

\displaystyle+2U_{\omega}\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}\zeta+4\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}.

Analogously,

\displaystyle\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}\left(\frac{1}{1-2\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{2}}\right)^{2}+32\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 8\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+32\|{\mathbf{Q}}_{j}\|_{F}^{4}.

(154)

Similar to (
I.3
), the first term can be decomposed and bounded as,

\displaystyle 8\left\|\frac{\zeta}{KL}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{P}}_{j,\perp}\delta_{j,L}^{k}\phi(s_{j,0}^{k})(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\widetilde{A}_{j,L}^{k}}x_{j}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}+\frac{24\zeta^{2}}{K^{2}L^{2}}\left\|\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbf{b}}_{j,L}^{k}(\omega_{j}^{k})^{\top}\right\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}.

(155)

Plugging (
I.3
) back to (
I.3
), conditioning on
v_{0:t-\tau}
, and applying Lemma
I.1
, we get

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|{\mathbf{B}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{B}}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{j}^{k}\|^{2}+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32(\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\zeta)^{4}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 96U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(8\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}+128\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{3}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+4\tau^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\alpha^{2}+2{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32(\frac{U_{\delta}}{L(1-\gamma)}U_{\omega}\zeta)^{4}~~~~~\text{(applying Lemma \ref{lmm: jth to tth error bound})}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 768\tau^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\beta^{2}\zeta^{2}+12288\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{5}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{4}+384\tau^{2}U_{\omega}^{2}L_{*,1}^{2}B^{2}U_{\delta}^{2}\frac{\alpha^{2}\zeta^{2}}{L^{2}}

\displaystyle+192U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{\zeta^{2}}{KL^{2}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\|x_{t}^{k}\|^{2}+24U_{\omega}^{2}\frac{U_{\delta}^{2}}{L^{2}(1-\gamma)^{2}}\zeta^{2}+32\frac{U_{\delta}^{4}U_{\omega}^{4}\zeta^{4}}{L^{4}(1-\gamma)^{4}}.

This gives the second claimed bound.

∎

I.4
Perturbation between Original Chains and Auxiliary Chains

Lemma I.7
.

Let
f:{\mathcal{S}}\times({\mathcal{A}}\times{\mathcal{S}})^{L}\to{\mathbb{R}}
. For any
t
and any agent
k
, let
s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,s_{t,L}^{k}
denote the sampled trajectory for critic, and

\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k}
denote the hypothetical trajectory sampled from the auxiliary chain associated with the critic.
Suppose that
f
takes the form

f(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,s_{t,L}^{k})=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}f_{\ell}(s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}).

Then, for any fixed
\tau
, the following results hold:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(s_{t,0}^{k},...,s_{t,L}^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{2}{(1-\rho^{L})(1-\rho)}+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|],

where
\sup\|f_{\ell}\|
denotes the sup of function
f_{\ell}
.

Proof.

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(s_{t,0}^{k},...,s_{t,L}^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}f_{\ell}(s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}f_{\ell}(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f_{\ell}(s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})\right]-\gamma^{\ell}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f_{\ell}(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})\phi(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})).

(156)

By Lemma
I.9
,

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|].

Plugging this back to (
I.4
),

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|\left(2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-1,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-1,L)}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]\right).

(157)

Again, by Lemma
I.9
, we have

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-1,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-1,L)}^{k}(\cdot))\leq\rho^{L}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-2,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-2,L)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-1}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]~~~~\text{(by choosing $\tau^{\prime}=\tau-1$)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{L}\Bigg(\rho^{L}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-3,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-3,L)}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-2}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]\Bigg)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-1}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]~~~\text{(by choosing $\tau^{\prime}=\tau-2$)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{2L}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-3,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-3,L)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle+\rho^{L}\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}[\|\theta_{t-2}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\||v_{0:t-\tau}]+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}[\|\theta_{t-1}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\||v_{0:t-\tau}]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{(t-(t-\tau)-1)L}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau:(t-\tau,L)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau:(t-\tau,L)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle+\sum_{m=1}^{t-(t-\tau)-1}(\rho^{L})^{m-1}\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-m}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{\tau-1}(\rho^{L})^{m-1}\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-m}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|].

In addition, for any
m=1,\cdots,\tau-1
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t-m}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-m-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|]\leq{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

Thus, Eq.(
I.4
) can be further bounded as

\displaystyle\eqref{eqn: l-summation to single recursion}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{2}{(1-\rho^{L})(1-\rho)}+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

∎

By following the same line of analysis, analogous results can be established for the sampled trajectory used in the actor update and its corresponding hypothetical trajectory.

Corollary 2
.

Let
f:{\mathcal{S}}\times({\mathcal{A}}\times{\mathcal{S}})^{L}\to{\mathbb{R}}
. For any
t
and any agent
k
, let
\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,\widehat{s}_{t,L}^{k}
denote the sampled trajectory for actor, and

\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{a}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{s}_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,\bar{s}_{t,L}^{k}
denote the hypothetical trajectory sampled from the auxiliary chain associated with the actor.
Suppose that
f
takes the form

f(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,1}^{k},\cdots,\widehat{s}_{t,L}^{k})=\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}f_{\ell}(\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}).

Then, for any fixed
\tau
, the following results hold:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\widehat{s}_{t,L}^{k})\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\bar{s}_{t,L}^{k})\phi(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{2}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}+\frac{2-\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|],

Lemma I.8
.

Under the same setup as in Lemma
I.7
, but replacing
\phi(s_{t,0}^{k})
with a general function
h(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k})
where
\sup\|h\|<\infty
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(s_{t,0}^{k},...,s_{t,L}^{k})h(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\widetilde{s}_{t,L}^{k})h(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sup\|h\|\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{2}{(1-\rho^{L})(1-\rho)}+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

Proof.

The proof follows identically to Lemma
I.7
, with the only difference that the TV distance now involves the 5-tuple
(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})
instead of the 4-tuple. Specifically, in (
I.4
), we bound

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f_{\ell}(s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k})h(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f_{\ell}(\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})h(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup\|h\|\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|

\displaystyle\quad\cdot 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k})).

By Lemma
I.9
, we have

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau}^{k}\|].

The rest of the proof follows identically to Lemma
I.7
, with
\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}
replacing
\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}
, giving the final bound with
\frac{2}{(1-\rho^{L})(1-\rho)}+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}
.
∎

Corollary 3
.

Under the same setup as in Corollary
2
but replacing
\phi(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k})
with a general function
h(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,0}^{k})
where
\sup\|h\|<\infty
, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\widehat{s}_{t,L}^{k})h(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,0}^{k})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}\left[f(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},...,\bar{s}_{t,L}^{k})h(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{a}_{t,0}^{k})\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\sup\|h\|\max_{\ell\in\{0,\cdots,L-1\}}\sup\|f_{\ell}\|}{1-\gamma}\left(\frac{2}{(1-\gamma^{L})(1-\gamma)}+\frac{3-2\gamma}{1-\gamma}\right)L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\sum_{i=t-\tau}^{t-1}{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau}[\|\theta_{i+1}^{k}-\theta_{i}^{k}\|].

Lemma I.9
.

Fix
\tau^{\prime}
.
For any
t
and
\ell
such that
t\geq\tau^{\prime}
and
\ell\in\{0,\dots,L-1\}
, we have

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))\leq\rho d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))\leq\rho^{\ell+1}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot))
