\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

Proof.

With a little abuse of notation, we use
{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}
to denote the expectation taken over the randomness of
\theta_{t}^{k}
conditioning on
v_{0:t-\tau^{\prime}}
. We have

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\int_{s_{\ell+1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\left|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0},ds_{\ell},a_{\ell},ds_{\ell+1})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0},ds_{\ell},a_{\ell},ds_{\ell+1})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\int_{s_{\ell+1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})P^{k}(ds_{\ell+1}|ds_{\ell},a_{\ell})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})P^{k}(ds_{\ell+1}|ds_{\ell},a_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\bigg|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\bigg|.

(158)

For the first term in (
I.4
), we have,

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot)).

(159)

For the second term in (
I.4
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\bigg|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\left|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\right|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\bigg|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\left|{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\right|

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\bigg|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}))

\displaystyle\qquad+L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|{\theta_{t}^{k}}-{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}\|],

(160)

where the last inequality follows from Assumption
3
.

It remains to bound
2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}))
.

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{s_{\ell}}\left|\int_{s_{\ell-1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}{\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1},ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1},ds_{\ell}|ds_{0})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{s_{\ell}}\Bigg|\int_{s_{\ell-1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell-1}|ds_{\ell-1})P^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{U}}_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell-1}|ds_{\ell-1})P^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s_{\ell}}\int_{s_{\ell-1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1}|ds_{0}))\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell-1}|ds_{\ell-1})P^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle+\int_{s_{\ell}}\Bigg|\int_{s_{\ell-1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(ds_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})(\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell-1}|ds_{\ell-1})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell-1}|ds_{\ell-1}))P^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{\ell-1},a_{\ell-1})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})\otimes\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k},{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0})\otimes\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})+L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\rho d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0}))+L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|.~~~~(\text{by Dobrushin condition})

Recognizing the recursive structure of the above inequality, we recursively unroll it to obtain

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{\ell}|ds_{0}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{\ell-1}|ds_{0}))+\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{\ell}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(s_{0}|ds_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{0}|ds_{0}))+\sum_{m=0}^{\ell-1}\rho^{m}\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\rho}\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|,

(161)

where the last inequality follows from conditioning on the initial state and the fact that
s_{0}
is the shared initial state at round
t
by both the original and auxiliary critic chains. Consequently, the induced distributions at time 0 are identical point masses at
s_{0}
, yielding

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{0}|s_{0}),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{0}|s_{0}))=0.

Putting (
I.4
) back to (
I.4
),

\displaystyle\text{\eqref{eqn: second term of the 4tuple decomp}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|]+L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|{\theta_{t}^{k}}-{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}\|]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

Finally, putting the above inequality and (
I.4
) back to (
I.4
), we have,

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\int_{s_{\ell+1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\left|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0},a_{0},ds_{\ell},a_{\ell},ds_{\ell+1})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0},a_{0},ds_{\ell},a_{\ell},ds_{\ell+1})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\int_{s_{\ell+1}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|P^{k}(ds_{\ell+1}|ds_{\ell},a_{\ell})

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})P^{k}(ds_{\ell+1}|ds_{\ell},a_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}^{k}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})

\displaystyle+\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\left[\text{ Term 1}+\text{ Term 2}\right].

(162)

For Term 1 in (
I.4
), integrating out
a_{0},s_{\ell},a_{\ell}
gives 1, so

\displaystyle\text{Term 1}=\int_{s_{0}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\Bigg|=2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot)).

(163)

For Term 2 in (
I.4
), we add and subtract
\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0}){\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|
to separate the policy drift on
a_{0}
from the transition kernel drift:

\displaystyle\text{Term 2}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\left|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})\right|{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|

\displaystyle+\int_{s_{0}}\sum_{a_{0}}\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})\Bigg|{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})|

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle=:

\displaystyle\text{ Piece A}+\text{ Piece B}.

(164)

For Piece A, integrating out
s_{\ell},a_{\ell}
gives 1, and applying Assumption
3
(c),

\displaystyle\text{Piece A}\leq\int_{s_{0}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})\sum_{a_{0}}\left|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})\right|\leq L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\||v_{0:t-\tau^{\prime}}].

(165)

For Piece B, for each fixed
(s_{0},a_{0})
, the inner absolute value has the same structure as the second term in the 4-tuple decomposition (
I.4
). Specifically, since
P^{k}(\cdot|s_{0},a_{0})
is the shared transition kernel and
(s_{0},a_{0})
is the shared starting point, the distribution of
s_{1}
is identical for both chains:

\displaystyle P^{k}(s_{1}|s_{0},a_{0})=\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{1}|s_{0},a_{0})=\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{1}|s_{0},a_{0}),

giving the base case:

\displaystyle d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{1}|s_{0},a_{0}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(s_{1}|s_{0},a_{0}))=0.

Applying the same Dobrushin recursion as in (
I.4
) from
\ell
down to 1, and using
\sum_{m=0}^{\ell-2}\rho^{m}\leq\frac{1}{1-\rho}
, we get for each fixed
(s_{0},a_{0})
,

\displaystyle\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s_{\ell}}\Bigg|\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})-\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\Bigg|

\displaystyle\qquad+\int_{s_{\ell}}\sum_{a_{\ell}}\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{\ell}|ds_{0},a_{0})\left|\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})-\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{\ell}|ds_{\ell})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|.

(166)

Integrating over
s_{0}
under
\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(ds_{0})
and summing over
a_{0}
under
\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a_{0}|ds_{0})
, which sums to 1, we get

\displaystyle\text{Piece B}\leq\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

Putting (
163
), (
165
), and (
I.4
) back to (
I.4
), we get

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(s_{t,0}^{k},a_{t,0}^{k},s_{t,\ell}^{k},a_{t,\ell}^{k},s_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widetilde{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widetilde{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\mathbb{P}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\mathbb{U}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

(167)

Next, we bound
2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))
.

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))=\int_{s}\left|{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(ds)-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(ds)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s}\left|\int_{s^{\prime}}\sum_{a}{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds,a,ds^{\prime})-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds,a,ds^{\prime})\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s}\left|\int_{s^{\prime}}\sum_{a}{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s}\Bigg|\int_{s^{\prime}}\sum_{a}{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)\Bigg|

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s}\Bigg|\int_{s^{\prime}}\sum_{a}{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}\int_{s}\int_{s^{\prime}}\sum_{a}\Bigg|{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(ds)\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}(a|ds)P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|ds,a)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|]+2d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}\otimes\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}\otimes P^{k},{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}\otimes\pi_{\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}}\otimes P^{k})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{t}^{k},t-\tau^{\prime}}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|]+2\rho d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle~~~~(\text{by Dobrushin condition})

Unrolling the above to
(t,0)
, we get,

\displaystyle d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\rho^{\ell+1}d_{TV}({\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot),{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{1}{1-\rho}\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

∎

Corollary 4
.

Fix
\tau^{\prime}
.
For any
t
and
\ell
such that
t\geq\tau^{\prime}
and
\ell\in\{0,\dots,L-1\}
, we have

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\bar{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\bar{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2d_{TV}(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{2-\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle 2d_{TV}(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\widehat{s}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,0}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\widehat{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\bar{s}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{a}_{t,0}^{k},\bar{s}_{t,\ell}^{k},\bar{a}_{t,\ell}^{k},\bar{s}_{t,\ell+1}^{k}))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\leq 2d_{TV}(\widehat{\mathbb{P}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:t}^{k}(\cdot))+\frac{3-2\rho}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle d_{TV}(\widehat{{\mathbb{P}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{{\mathbb{U}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))\leq\rho d_{TV}(\widehat{{\mathbb{P}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{{\mathbb{U}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

\displaystyle d_{TV}(\widehat{{\mathbb{P}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{{\mathbb{U}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,\ell+1)}^{k}(\cdot))\leq\rho^{\ell+1}d_{TV}(\widehat{{\mathbb{P}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot),\bar{{\mathbb{U}}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}:(t,0)}^{k}(\cdot))

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{1}{2}\frac{1}{1-\rho}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|{\mathbb{E}}_{t-\tau^{\prime}}[\|\theta_{t}^{k}-\theta_{t-\tau^{\prime}}^{k}\|].

I.5
Proofs of Preliminary Lemmas

Proof of Lemma
D.6

Proof.

The TD fixed point
z_{\theta}^{k,*}
satisfies

A_{L,\theta}^{k}z_{\theta}^{k,*}+\bar{b}_{L,\theta}^{k}=0,

where

\displaystyle A_{L,\theta}^{k}=\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}]

\displaystyle\bar{b}_{L,\theta}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{\theta}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

The expectations are taken over
{s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta}^{k},(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta}\otimes P^{k}~\forall\ell\in[0,L-1]}
. Since
\mathbf{B}^{*}
is a linear transformation on the feature representation, the fixed point in the projected coordinates satisfies

A^{k}_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},i}{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{i}},\mathbf{B}^{*})+b^{k}_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},i}=0,

where
A^{k}_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},i}={\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{i}}[(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\phi(s^{(L)})-(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}]=(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*}
, and
b^{k}_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},i}={\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{i}}[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\phi(s^{(0)})]=(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}
.

For any
\theta_{1},\theta_{2}
, we have:

\displaystyle{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{1}},\mathbf{B}^{*})=-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}=-((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}{\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}};

\displaystyle{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{2}},\mathbf{B}^{*})=-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k}=-((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}{\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}}.

\displaystyle\|{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{1}},\mathbf{B}^{*})-{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{2}},\mathbf{B}^{*})\|=\|-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}-(-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k}-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}+(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k}-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}\|+\|(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k})^{-1}b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}\|\|b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k}-b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}\|+\|(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},2}^{k})^{-1}-(A_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k})^{-1}\|\|b_{L,\mathbf{B}^{*},1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}-(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|

(168)

\displaystyle+\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}-((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\left\|{(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}}\right\|\|\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})-((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})\|\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\left\|{(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}}\right\|\|\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle+\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}(A_{L,1}^{k}-A_{L,2}^{k})\mathbf{B}^{*})\|\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|\|(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|.

(169)

By Assumption
1
, the
A_{L,i}^{k}
’s are negative definite and the largest eigenvalue is upper bounded by
-\lambda L
. Moreover, by Poincaré Separation Theorem, since
\mathbf{B}^{*}
has orthonormal columns, then the eigenvalues of
(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*}
are upper bounded by the eigenvalues of
A_{L,i}^{k}
, meaning that the largest eigenvalue of
(\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*}
is upper bounded by
-\lambda L
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\|((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1}\|=\max_{i}|\lambda_{i}(((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})^{-1})|=\max_{i}|\frac{1}{\lambda_{i}((\mathbf{B}^{*})^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B}^{*})}|\leq(\lambda L)^{-1}.

(170)

It can be easily shown that
\|\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}\|\leq\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}
.
Then, (
169
) can be bounded by:

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda L}\|\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}(\lambda L)^{-2}\|A_{L,1}^{k}-A_{L,2}^{k}\|.

We first bound
\|A_{L,1}^{k}-A_{L,2}^{k}\|
.
For
i\in\{1,2\}
and
0\leq\ell\leq L-1
, let
\mathsf{P}_{i,\ell}^{k}
denote the trajectory

(s^{(0)},a^{(0)},s^{(1)},\ldots,a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})
generated from

s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{i}}^{k}
and
(a^{(j)},s^{(j+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{i}}\otimes P^{k}
for
0\leq j\leq\ell
.

We have

\displaystyle\|A_{L,\theta_{1}}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{2}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\Big({\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k}}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})\big(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)})\big)^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})\big(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)})\big)^{\top}\right]\Big)\Bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\Bigg\|{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k}}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})\big(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)})\big)^{\top}\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})\big(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)})\big)^{\top}\right]\Bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}d_{TV}\big(\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k},\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}\big),

where the last inequality follows from the definition of total variation distance and

\sup_{s^{(0)},s^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)}}\left\|\phi(s^{(0)})\big(\gamma\phi(s^{(\ell+1)})-\phi(s^{(\ell)})\big)^{\top}\right\|\leq 1+\gamma\leq 2.

Moreover, since the transition kernel
P^{k}
is shared under
\theta_{1}
and
\theta_{2}
, the path-measure difference only comes from the initial stationary distributions and the policies along the trajectory. Thus,

\displaystyle d_{TV}\big(\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k},\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}\big)

\displaystyle\leq d_{TV}\big(\mu_{\theta_{1}}^{k},\mu_{\theta_{2}}^{k}\big)+(\ell+1)\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}d_{TV}\big(\pi_{\theta_{1}}(\cdot|s),\pi_{\theta_{2}}(\cdot|s)\big)

\displaystyle\leq C_{\mu}\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|+\frac{\ell+1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|,

where the last inequality follows from Lemma
D.8
and Assumption
3
. Plugging this into the previous display gives

\displaystyle\|A_{L,\theta_{1}}^{k}-A_{L,\theta_{2}}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq 4\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(C_{\mu}+\frac{\ell+1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|.

We next bound
\|\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}\|
. Using the same partial trajectory laws
\mathsf{P}_{i,\ell}^{k}
as above, we have

\displaystyle\|\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}\|

\displaystyle=\left\|\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left({\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k}}\left[R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]-{\mathbb{E}}_{\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}}\left[R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right]\right)\right\|

\displaystyle\leq 2U_{r}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}d_{TV}\big(\mathsf{P}_{1,\ell}^{k},\mathsf{P}_{2,\ell}^{k}\big)

\displaystyle\leq 2U_{r}\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}\left(C_{\mu}+\frac{\ell+1}{2}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|.

Finally,

\displaystyle\|{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{1}},\mathbf{B}^{*})-{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta_{2}},\mathbf{B}^{*})\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda L}\|\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}\|+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}(\lambda L)^{-2}\|A_{L,1}^{k}-A_{L,2}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\lambda L}\left(\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|{\theta_{1}}-{\theta_{2}}\|+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}(\lambda L)^{-2}\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|{\theta_{1}}-{\theta_{2}}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle L_{*,1}\|{\theta_{1}}-{\theta_{2}}\|,

where

L_{*,1}=\frac{1}{\lambda L}\left(\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)+\frac{U_{r}}{1-\gamma}(\lambda L)^{-2}\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right).

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.7

Proof.

Recall

\displaystyle A_{L,\theta}^{k}=\mathbb{E}_{\theta}[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}]

\displaystyle\bar{b}_{L,\theta}^{k}={\mathbb{E}}_{\theta}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right].

For ease of notation, write
A_{L,i}^{k}:=A_{L,\theta_{i}}^{k}
and
\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}:=\bar{b}_{L,\theta_{i}}^{k}
for
i\in\{1,2\}
. For any orthonormal
\mathbf{B}
,

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}{\omega}^{k,*}({\theta}_{i},\mathbf{B})=-\nabla_{\theta}\left[(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-(\nabla_{\theta}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1})\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}-(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\nabla_{\theta}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\nabla_{\theta}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}-(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\nabla_{\theta}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}(\nabla_{\theta}A_{L,i}^{k})\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}-(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\nabla_{\theta}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}.

The constant terms
\mathbf{B}
and
\mathbf{B}^{\top}
are bounded. From (
170
), we know

\|(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\|\leq(\lambda L)^{-1}.

Moreover, the inverse map is Lipschitz:

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}-(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,1}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\mathbf{B}^{\top}(A_{L,2}^{k}-A_{L,1}^{k})\mathbf{B}(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,2}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\lambda L)^{-2}\|A_{L,2}^{k}-A_{L,1}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(\lambda L)^{-2}\left(\frac{4C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{2L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|,

where the last inequality follows from the proof of Lemma
D.6
. The same proof also gives

\displaystyle\|\bar{b}_{L,1}^{k}-\bar{b}_{L,2}^{k}\|

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{2U_{r}C_{\mu}}{1-\gamma}+\frac{U_{r}L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|}{(1-\gamma)^{2}}\right)\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|,

and
\|\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}\|\leq U_{r}/(1-\gamma)
.

It remains to control the derivative terms. Since

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}A_{L,i}^{k}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\theta}{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{i}}\left[\phi(s^{(0)})(\gamma^{L}\phi(s^{(L)})-\phi(s^{(0)}))^{\top}\right],

\displaystyle\nabla_{\theta}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}

\displaystyle=\nabla_{\theta}{\mathbb{E}}_{\theta_{i}}\left[\sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\gamma^{\ell}R(s^{(\ell)},a^{(\ell)})\phi(s^{(0)})\right],

where the expectations are taken over

s^{(0)}\sim\mu_{\theta_{i}}^{k}
and

(a^{(\ell)},s^{(\ell+1)})\sim\pi_{\theta_{i}}\otimes P^{k}
for all
\ell\in[0,L-1]
.
Assumption
3
and the additional smoothness condition in this lemma imply that

\nabla_{\theta}A_{L,i}^{k}
and
\nabla_{\theta}\bar{b}_{L,i}^{k}
are both bounded and Lipschitz in
\theta
.
Combining these bounds with the displayed formula for
\nabla_{\theta}\omega^{k,*}
and the Lipschitz bound on

(\mathbf{B}^{\top}A_{L,i}^{k}\mathbf{B})^{-1}
shows that

\nabla_{\theta}\omega^{k,*}(\theta,\mathbf{B})
is Lipschitz in
\theta
.
Specializing to
\mathbf{B}=\mathbf{B}^{*}
gives the claimed constant
L_{s,1}
.

∎

Proof of Lemma
D.8

Proof.

We prove each bullet point.

•

Since here
\mu^{k}_{{\theta_{1}}},\mu^{k}_{{\theta_{2}}}
are stationary distributions induced by the Markov chain,
n=\infty
.
By Theorem 3.1 of
[
26
]
, we have

d_{TV}(\mu^{k}_{{\theta_{1}}},\mu^{k}_{{\theta_{2}}})\leq(\widehat{n}+\frac{1}{1-\rho})\|{\mathcal{K}}_{1}-{\mathcal{K}}_{2}\|,

where
{\mathcal{K}}_{i}(s,ds^{\prime})=\sum_{a}P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|s,a)\pi_{{\theta_{i}}}(a|s),\widehat{n}=\lceil{\log_{\rho}m^{-1}}\rceil.

\displaystyle\|{\mathcal{K}}_{1}-{\mathcal{K}}_{2}\|=\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\|{\mathcal{K}}_{1}(s,\cdot)-{\mathcal{K}}_{2}(s,\cdot)\|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\int_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left|\sum_{a}P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|s,a)\pi_{{\theta_{1}}}(a|s)-\sum_{a}P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|s,a)\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}(a|s)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\int_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\left|\sum_{a}P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|s,a)\left(\pi_{{\theta_{1}}}(a|s)-\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}(a|s)\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{s\in{\mathcal{S}}}\int_{s^{\prime}\in{\mathcal{S}}}\sum_{a}P^{k}(ds^{\prime}|s,a)\left|\pi_{{\theta_{1}}}(a|s)-\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}(a|s)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle L_{\pi}|{\mathcal{A}}|\|\theta_{1}-\theta_{2}\|.

•

\displaystyle d_{TV}(\mu_{{\theta_{1}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{1}}},\mu_{{\theta_{2}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{2}}})\leq d_{TV}(\mu_{{\theta_{1}}}^{k},\mu_{{\theta_{2}}}^{k})+d_{TV}(\pi_{{\theta_{1}}},\pi_{{\theta_{2}}})

\displaystyle=|{\mathcal{A}}|L_{\pi}(\lceil{\log_{\rho}m^{-1}}\rceil+\frac{1}{1-\rho})\|{\theta_{2}}-{\theta_{1}}\|+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{a}|\pi_{{\theta_{1}}}(a|s)-\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}(a|s)|

\displaystyle=|{\mathcal{A}}|L_{\pi}(\lceil{\log_{\rho}m^{-1}}\rceil+\frac{1}{1-\rho})\|{\theta_{2}}-{\theta_{1}}\|+\frac{1}{2}|{\mathcal{A}}|L_{\pi}\|{\theta_{2}}-{\theta_{1}}\|.

•

\displaystyle d_{TV}(\mu_{{\theta_{1}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{1}}}\otimes P^{k},\mu_{{\theta_{2}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}\otimes P^{k})\leq d_{TV}(\mu_{{\theta_{1}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{1}}},\mu_{{\theta_{2}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{2}}})+d_{TV}(P^{k},P^{k})

\displaystyle=d_{TV}(\mu_{\theta_{1}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{1}}},\mu_{{\theta_{2}}}^{k}\otimes\pi_{{\theta_{2}}}).

∎

Appendix J
Detailed Experimental Setup

J.1
Environment and Heterogeneity Construction

We use Gymnasium’s
Hopper-v5
with the MuJoCo backend as the underlying environment. The only environment-side modification is reducing the standard healthy-reward bonus from
1.0
to
0.2
, so that the reward signal is dominated by forward progress rather than the alive bonus. We use
max_episode_steps
=1000
and the default Hopper termination criteria.

For each client, we wrap the environment with an
action map
: before an action is passed to the MuJoCo simulator, its three joint-torque coordinates are permuted and then multiplied by a client-specific positive scale. Thus, each client’s policy emits actions in its own permuted and scaled coordinate system, while the underlying physics receives the mapped action. The observation space and MuJoCo dynamics are shared across clients, but the same policy action can produce different physical torques and hence different next-state distributions. This matches the heterogeneous-transition setting of our theory, where each agent has its own transition kernel
P^{k}
.

The grouped setup uses two action maps: permutation
[1,0,2]
with scale
0.7
, and permutation
[2,1,0]
with scale
1.5
, with three clients assigned to each map. The
6-unique
setup uses all six permutations of
[0,1,2]
paired with distinct scales
\{1.0,0.8,1.2,0.7,1.5,0.9\}
, one client per permutation-scale pair. The OOD transfer experiment in Section
6.2
uses the identity permutation with an out-of-range action scale of
0.5
, which is below the pretraining range.

J.2
Algorithms

All algorithms share the same network architecture: a
2
-layer MLP shared
trunk
of width
256
with ReLU activations, followed by client-side output components, including a linear actor head producing the action mean, a linear critic head, and a diagonal
\log\sigma
parameter, the log-standard-deviation of the Gaussian policy’s exploration noise. The algorithms differ only in which parameters are synchronized across clients:

•

Single PPO
: each client trains its full network independently, with no cross-client communication.

•

FedAvg PPO
: every
10
episode rounds, all parameters are averaged across clients.

•

FedPer PPO
(ours): every
10
episode rounds, only the trunk parameters are averaged. The actor heads, critic heads, and
\log\sigma
parameters remain personalized.

J.3
PPO Hyperparameters

We use a Hopper-tuned PPO recipe taken from a public reference implementation, identical across all three algorithms and across all experiments (Table
1
). We deliberately do not re-tune per algorithm; any reported difference is therefore attributable to the federated synchronization scheme rather than hyperparameter optimization.

J.4

All experiments are run on a MacBook Air with an Apple M2 chip, using the PyTorch CPU backend without GPU acceleration. Wall-clock totals for the experiments reported in this paper are listed in Table

.

The pretrained trunk used in the foundation-transfer ablation comes from the post-final-sync FedPer checkpoint of the
6-unique
run. Because the trunk is synchronized at episode round
5000
before the final checkpoint is written, the trunk is identical across all six clients of that run.

Appendix K
Additional Experimental Results

K.1
Heterogeneity Training Dynamics

Figure 3
:
Training curves over per-client environment steps for the heterogeneity ablation in Section
6.1
. Bands show
\pm 1
standard deviation over
3
seeds; the dashed vertical line marks the
0.4\,\text{M}
common-budget reporting point used in Fig.
1
.

A natural concern about Fig.
1
is that, under a fixed-episode training budget, stronger policies may collect more environment interactions because Hopper episodes terminate early after falls. Fig.
3
addresses this by re-plotting the same runs against per-client environment steps and truncating all methods to the smallest shared horizon. The relative ordering is preserved under this env-step-fair view, confirming that FedPer’s advantage in Fig.
1
reflects faster learning per environment interaction rather than a longer-episode confound.
